package authorization

import "context"

// RoleAssignmentRepository defines persistence operations
// related to user role assignments.
type RoleAssignmentRepository interface {
	GetActiveUserRoles(
		ctx context.Context,
		userID string,
	) ([]UserRole, error)

	AssignRole(
		ctx context.Context,
		assignment UserRole,
	) error

	RemoveRole(
		ctx context.Context,
		assignmentID string,
	) error
}

// PermissionOverrideRepository defines persistence operations
// related to user-specific allow/deny overrides.
type PermissionOverrideRepository interface {
	GetActiveUserOverrides(
		ctx context.Context,
		userID string,
	) ([]UserPermissionOverride, error)

	AddOverride(
		ctx context.Context,
		override UserPermissionOverride,
	) error

	RemoveOverride(
		ctx context.Context,
		overrideID string,
	) error
}

package authorization

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
)

func TestAuthorizationPostgreSQL(t *testing.T) {

	if strings.ToLower(
		os.Getenv("AUTHORIZATION_INTEGRATION_TEST"),
	) != "true" {
		t.Skip(
			"PostgreSQL integration test is disabled. " +
				"Set AUTHORIZATION_INTEGRATION_TEST=true to run it.",
		)
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(
		context.Background(),
		20*time.Second,
	)
	defer cancel()

	dsn := os.Getenv(
		"AUTHORIZATION_TEST_DATABASE_URL",
	)

	if dsn == "" {
		dsn = "postgres://account:account_password@localhost:5432/account?sslmode=disable"
	}

	pool, err := pgxpool.New(
		ctx,
		dsn,
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to connect to PostgreSQL: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	defer pool.Close()

	if err := pool.Ping(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to ping PostgreSQL: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	db := postgres.NewClient(pool)

	repository := NewRepository(db)

	service := NewAuthorizationService(
		repository,
		repository,
		repository,
		repository,
		repository,
	)

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Test users
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	user1ID := createTestUser(t, ctx, pool)
	user2ID := createTestUser(t, ctx, pool)
	user3ID := createTestUser(t, ctx, pool)

	defer cleanupAuthorizationTestData(
		ctx,
		pool,
		user1ID,
		user2ID,
		user3ID,
	)

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Existing seeded role and permission
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	role, err := repository.GetRoleByCode(
		ctx,
		RoleProjectManager,
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to load project manager role: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	permission, err := repository.GetPermissionByCode(
		ctx,
		PermissionProjectUpdate,
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to load project.update permission: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Scope hierarchy
	//
	// Ã˜Â³Ã˜Â§Ã˜Â²Ã™â€¦Ã˜Â§Ã™â€ 
	//   Ã¢â€Å“Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Ã™Â¾Ã˜Â±Ã™Ë†ÃšËœÃ™â€¡ Ã›Â±
	//   Ã¢â€â€Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Ã™Â¾Ã˜Â±Ã™Ë†ÃšËœÃ™â€¡ Ã›Â²
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	organizationID := createScope(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
		"organization",
		"organization",
	)

	project1ID := createScopeWithParent(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
		"project",
		"project",
		organizationID,
	)

	project2ID := createScopeWithParent(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
		"project",
		"project",
		organizationID,
	)

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// User 1
	//
	// Role:
	// Ã™â€¦Ã˜Â¯Ã›Å’Ã˜Â± Ã™Â¾Ã˜Â±Ã™Ë†ÃšËœÃ™â€¡
	//
	// Scope:
	// Ã˜Â³Ã˜Â§Ã˜Â²Ã™â€¦Ã˜Â§Ã™â€ 
	//
	// Expected:
	// Project 1 = ALLOW
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	assignRole(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
		user1ID,
		role.ID,
		organizationID,
	)

	result, err := service.Authorize(
		ctx,
		AuthorizationRequest{
			UserID:       user1ID,
			Permission:   PermissionProjectUpdate,
			ResourceType: "project",
			ResourceID: stringPointer(
				"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001",
			),
			ScopeID: stringPointer(project1ID),
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"user 1 project 1 authorization failed: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	if result.Decision != AuthorizationDecisionAllow {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected user 1 project 1 access to be allowed, got %s (%s)",
			result.Decision,
			result.Reason,
		)
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// User 1 explicit deny
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	addOverride(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
		user1ID,
		permission.ID,
		PermissionEffectDeny,
		project1ID,
	)

	result, err = service.Authorize(
		ctx,
		AuthorizationRequest{
			UserID:       user1ID,
			Permission:   PermissionProjectUpdate,
			ResourceType: "project",
			ResourceID: stringPointer(
				"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001",
			),
			ScopeID: stringPointer(project1ID),
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"user 1 deny override authorization failed: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	if result.Decision != AuthorizationDecisionDeny {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected explicit deny to win, got %s (%s)",
			result.Decision,
			result.Reason,
		)
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// User 2
	//
	// Role:
	// Ã™â€¦Ã˜Â¯Ã›Å’Ã˜Â± Ã™Â¾Ã˜Â±Ã™Ë†ÃšËœÃ™â€¡
	//
	// Scope:
	// Ã™ÂÃ™â€šÃ˜Â· Ã™Â¾Ã˜Â±Ã™Ë†ÃšËœÃ™â€¡ Ã›Â±
	//
	// Project 1 = ALLOW
	// Project 2 = DENY
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	assignRole(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
		user2ID,
		role.ID,
		project1ID,
	)

	result, err = service.Authorize(
		ctx,
		AuthorizationRequest{
			UserID:       user2ID,
			Permission:   PermissionProjectUpdate,
			ResourceType: "project",
			ResourceID: stringPointer(
				"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001",
			),
			ScopeID: stringPointer(project1ID),
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"user 2 project 1 authorization failed: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	if result.Decision != AuthorizationDecisionAllow {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected user 2 project 1 access to be allowed, got %s (%s)",
			result.Decision,
			result.Reason,
		)
	}

	result, err = service.Authorize(
		ctx,
		AuthorizationRequest{
			UserID:       user2ID,
			Permission:   PermissionProjectUpdate,
			ResourceType: "project",
			ResourceID: stringPointer(
				"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000002",
			),
			ScopeID: stringPointer(project2ID),
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"user 2 project 2 authorization failed: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	if result.Decision != AuthorizationDecisionDeny {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected user 2 sibling project access to be denied, got %s (%s)",
			result.Decision,
			result.Reason,
		)
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// User 3
	//
	// No role.
	// Explicit ALLOW on project 2.
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	addOverride(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
		user3ID,
		permission.ID,
		PermissionEffectAllow,
		project2ID,
	)

	result, err = service.Authorize(
		ctx,
		AuthorizationRequest{
			UserID:       user3ID,
			Permission:   PermissionProjectUpdate,
			ResourceType: "project",
			ResourceID: stringPointer(
				"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000002",
			),
			ScopeID: stringPointer(project2ID),
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"user 3 allow override authorization failed: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	if result.Decision != AuthorizationDecisionAllow {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected explicit allow to grant access, got %s (%s)",
			result.Decision,
			result.Reason,
		)
	}

	t.Log(
		"PostgreSQL Authorization integration tests passed.",
	)
}

func createScope(
	t *testing.T,
	ctx context.Context,
	pool *pgxpool.Pool,
	scopeType string,
	resourceType string,
) string {

	var scopeID string
	var resourceID string

		ctx,
		`SELECT gen_random_uuid()::text`,
	).Scan(&resourceID)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to create resource id: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	err = pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		INSERT INTO authorization_scopes
		(
			scope_type,
			resource_type,
			resource_id,
			name,
			description,
			is_active
		)
		VALUES
		(
			$1,
			$2,
			$3::uuid,
			'Authorization Integration Test',
			'Temporary integration test scope',
			TRUE
		)
		RETURNING id::text
		`,
		scopeType,
		resourceType,
		resourceID,
	).Scan(&scopeID)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to create scope: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	return scopeID
}

func cleanupAuthorizationTestData(
	ctx context.Context,
	pool *pgxpool.Pool,
	user1ID string,
	user2ID string,
	user3ID string,
) {

	userIDs := []string{
		user1ID,
		user2ID,
		user3ID,
	}

	for _, userID := range userIDs {

		_, _ = pool.Exec(
			ctx,
			`
			DELETE FROM authorization_user_permission_overrides
			WHERE user_id = $1::uuid
			`,
			userID,
		)

		_, _ = pool.Exec(
			ctx,
			`
			DELETE FROM authorization_user_roles
			WHERE user_id = $1::uuid
			`,
			userID,
		)

		_, _ = pool.Exec(
			ctx,
			`
			DELETE FROM users
			WHERE id = $1::uuid
			`,
			userID,
		)
	}

	_, _ = pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		DELETE FROM authorization_scopes
		WHERE name = 'Authorization Integration Test'
		`,
	)
}

package authorization

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
)

// AuthorizationService evaluates role, permission, override and scope.
// Business policies are intentionally outside this service for now.
type AuthorizationService struct {
	RoleRepository               RoleRepository
	PermissionRepository         PermissionRepository
	RoleAssignmentRepository     RoleAssignmentRepository
	PermissionOverrideRepository PermissionOverrideRepository
	ScopeRepository              ScopeRepository
}

// NewAuthorizationService creates the authorization decision service.
func NewAuthorizationService(
	roleRepository RoleRepository,
	permissionRepository PermissionRepository,
	roleAssignmentRepository RoleAssignmentRepository,
	permissionOverrideRepository PermissionOverrideRepository,
	scopeRepository ScopeRepository,
) *AuthorizationService {
	return &AuthorizationService{
		RoleRepository:               roleRepository,
		PermissionRepository:         permissionRepository,
		RoleAssignmentRepository:     roleAssignmentRepository,
		PermissionOverrideRepository: permissionOverrideRepository,
		ScopeRepository:              scopeRepository,
	}
}

// Authorize evaluates the core Authorization model.
//
// Decision order:
//
// 1. Validate request
// 2. Load requested permission
// 3. Load user-specific overrides
// 4. DENY overrides always win
// 5. Matching ALLOW override can grant access
// 6. Load user roles
// 7. Check role permissions
// 8. Check role scope
// 9. Return ALLOW or DENY
//
// Business policies are intentionally not checked here.
func (s *AuthorizationService) Authorize(
	ctx context.Context,
	request AuthorizationRequest,
) (AuthorizationResult, error) {

	if s == nil {
		return AuthorizationResult{
			Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
			Reason:   "authorization service is nil",
		}, errors.New("authorization service is nil")
	}

	request.UserID = strings.TrimSpace(request.UserID)
	request.Permission = strings.TrimSpace(request.Permission)
	request.ResourceType = strings.TrimSpace(request.ResourceType)

	if request.UserID == "" {
		return AuthorizationResult{
			Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
			Reason:   "user id is required",
		}, nil
	}

	if request.Permission == "" {
		return AuthorizationResult{
			Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
			Reason:   "permission is required",
		}, nil
	}

	permission, err := s.PermissionRepository.GetPermissionByCode(
		ctx,
		request.Permission,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, ErrPermissionNotFound) {
			return AuthorizationResult{
				Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
				Reason:   "permission not found",
			}, nil
		}

		return AuthorizationResult{
			Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
			Reason:   "permission lookup failed",
		}, err
	}

	if !permission.IsActive {
		return AuthorizationResult{
			Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
			Reason:   "permission is inactive",
		}, nil
	}

	if request.ResourceType != "" &&
		request.ResourceType != permission.Resource {

		return AuthorizationResult{
			Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
			Reason:   "resource type does not match permission",
		}, nil
	}

	// ========================================================
	// User-specific overrides
	// ========================================================

	overrides, err := s.PermissionOverrideRepository.GetActiveUserOverrides(
		ctx,
		request.UserID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return AuthorizationResult{
			Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
			Reason:   "permission override lookup failed",
		}, err
	}

	allowOverrideFound := false

	for _, override := range overrides {

		if override.PermissionID != permission.ID {
			continue
		}

		allowed, err := s.ScopeRepository.IsScopeAllowed(
			ctx,
			override.ScopeID,
			request.ScopeID,
		)

		if err != nil {
			return AuthorizationResult{
				Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
				Reason:   "override scope lookup failed",
			}, err
		}

		if !allowed {
			continue
		}

		if override.Effect == PermissionEffectDeny {
			return AuthorizationResult{
				Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
				Reason:   "explicit user deny override",
			}, nil
		}

		if override.Effect == PermissionEffectAllow {
			allowOverrideFound = true
		}
	}

	if allowOverrideFound {
		return AuthorizationResult{
			Decision: AuthorizationDecisionAllow,
			Reason:   "explicit user allow override",
		}, nil
	}

	// ========================================================
	// User roles
	// ========================================================

	userRoles, err := s.RoleAssignmentRepository.GetActiveUserRoles(
		ctx,
		request.UserID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return AuthorizationResult{
			Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
			Reason:   "user roles lookup failed",
		}, err
	}

	for _, userRole := range userRoles {

		role, err := s.RoleRepository.GetRoleByID(
			ctx,
			userRole.RoleID,
		)

		if err != nil {
			if errors.Is(err, ErrRoleNotFound) {
				continue
			}

			return AuthorizationResult{
				Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
				Reason:   "role lookup failed",
			}, err
		}

		if !role.IsActive {
			continue
		}

		// Self role is valid only when the requested resource belongs
		// to the same user.
		if userRole.ScopeID == nil &&
			role.DefaultScopeType == ScopeTypeSelf {

			if request.ResourceID == nil ||
				*request.ResourceID != request.UserID {

				continue
			}
		}

		// Roles that require a concrete scope cannot operate without
		// either an assigned scope or a global default scope.
		if userRole.ScopeID == nil &&
			role.DefaultScopeType != ScopeTypeGlobal &&
			role.DefaultScopeType != ScopeTypeSelf {

			continue
		}

		scopeAllowed := true

		if userRole.ScopeID != nil {
			scopeAllowed, err = s.ScopeRepository.IsScopeAllowed(
				ctx,
				userRole.ScopeID,
				request.ScopeID,
			)

			if err != nil {
				return AuthorizationResult{
					Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
					Reason:   "role scope lookup failed",
				}, err
			}
		}

		if !scopeAllowed {
			continue
		}

		permissions, err := s.RoleRepository.ListPermissions(
			ctx,
			role.ID,
		)

		if err != nil {
			return AuthorizationResult{
				Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
				Reason:   "role permissions lookup failed",
			}, err
		}

		for _, rolePermission := range permissions {

			if rolePermission.ID != permission.ID {
				continue
			}

			return AuthorizationResult{
				Decision: AuthorizationDecisionAllow,
				Reason:   "permission granted by role",
			}, nil
		}
	}

	return AuthorizationResult{
		Decision: AuthorizationDecisionDeny,
		Reason:   "no matching role or permission",
	}, nil
}

package authorization

import (

// ============================================================
// Fake repositories
// ============================================================

type fakeRoleRepository struct {
	roles       map[string]Role
	permissions map[string][]Permission
}

func (f *fakeRoleRepository) GetRoleByID(
	_ context.Context,
	roleID string,
) (*Role, error) {

	role, ok := f.roles[roleID]
	if !ok {
		return nil, ErrRoleNotFound
	}

	return &role, nil
}

func (f *fakeRoleRepository) GetRoleByCode(
	_ context.Context,
	code string,
) (*Role, error) {

	for _, role := range f.roles {
		if role.Code == code {
			copy := role
			return &copy, nil
		}
	}

	return nil, ErrRoleNotFound
}

func (f *fakeRoleRepository) ListActiveRoles(
	_ context.Context,
) ([]Role, error) {

	result := make([]Role, 0)

	for _, role := range f.roles {
		if role.IsActive {
			result = append(result, role)
		}
	}

	return result, nil
}

func (f *fakeRoleRepository) ListPermissions(
	_ context.Context,
	roleID string,
) ([]Permission, error) {

	return f.permissions[roleID], nil
}

	permissions map[string]Permission
}

		if permission.ID == permissionID {

			return &copy, nil
		}
	}

	return nil, ErrPermissionNotFound
}

		}
	}

	return result, nil

	_ context.Context,

	return nil

	_ context.Context,
	assignmentID string,

		}
	}

	return nil
}

		Name:      "ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¾Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¹ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚ÂºÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¹Ãƒâ€¦Ã¢â‚¬Å“ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â±ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¹Ãƒâ€¦Ã¢â‚¬Å“ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚ÂºÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¾Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¹Ãƒâ€¦Ã¢â‚¬Å“ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â´ ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¾Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¾ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¹Ãƒâ€¦Ã¢â‚¬Å“ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â±ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¾Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¹ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã‚Â¬Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¹ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â¦ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…â€œÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¾Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã†â€™Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…Â¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¬ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â¡",
		IsActive:  true,
		CreatedAt: now,
		UpdatedAt: now,
	}

	roleRepository := &fakeRoleRepository{
		roles: map[string]Role{
			role.ID: role,
		},
		permissions: map[string][]Permission{
			role.ID: {
				permission,
			},
		},
	}

	permissionRepository := &fakePermissionRepository{
		permissions: map[string]Permission{
			permission.Code: permission,
		},
	}

	roleAssignmentRepository := &fakeRoleAssignmentRepository{
		assignments: []UserRole{
			{
				ID:        "assignment-1",
				UserID:    "user-1",
				RoleID:    role.ID,
				ScopeID:   stringPtr("project-scope-1"),
				IsActive:  true,
				StartsAt:  now.Add(-time.Hour),
				CreatedAt: now,
				UpdatedAt: now,
			},
		},
	}

	overrideRepository := &fakeOverrideRepository{}

	scopeRepository := &fakeScopeRepository{
		allowed: true,
	}

	service := NewAuthorizationService(
		roleRepository,
		permissionRepository,
		roleAssignmentRepository,
		overrideRepository,
		scopeRepository,
	)

	return service, overrideRepository
}

func TestAuthorize_AllowsRolePermission(t *testing.T) {

	service, _ := newAuthorizationTestService()

	result, err := service.Authorize(
		context.Background(),
		AuthorizationRequest{
			UserID:       "user-1",
			Permission:   PermissionProjectUpdate,
			ResourceType: "project",
			ScopeID:      stringPtr("project-scope-1"),
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}

	if result.Decision != AuthorizationDecisionAllow {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected allow, got %s",
			result.Decision,
		)
	}
}

func TestAuthorize_DeniesWithoutMatchingRolePermission(t *testing.T) {

	service, _ := newAuthorizationTestService()

	result, err := service.Authorize(
		context.Background(),
		AuthorizationRequest{
			UserID:       "user-1",
			Permission:   "wallet.read",
			ResourceType: "wallet",
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}

	if result.Decision != AuthorizationDecisionDeny {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected deny, got %s",
			result.Decision,
		)
	}
}

			PermissionID: "permission-1",

		context.Background(),

			ResourceType: "project",
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
	}

	if result.Decision != AuthorizationDecisionDeny {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected deny, got %s",
			result.Decision,
		)
	}
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: account-backend\internal\authorization\handler.go
SIZE: 1132 bytes
====================================================================================================
package authorization

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
)

// Handler exposes minimal Authorization endpoints.
//
// This endpoint is intentionally small and is used to verify
// that authentication and authorization are connected correctly.
type Handler struct{}

// NewHandler creates an Authorization handler.
func NewHandler() *Handler {
	return &Handler{}
}

// Me returns the authenticated user identity after Authorization
// middleware has successfully approved the request.
func (h *Handler) Me(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {

	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		writeAuthorizationError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)

		return
	}

	userID, ok := UserIDFromContext(
		r.Context(),
	)

	if !ok || userID == "" {
		writeAuthorizationError(
			w,
			http.StatusUnauthorized,
			"user identity is missing",
		)

		return
	}

	w.Header().Set(
		"Content-Type",
		"application/json",
	)

	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)

	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(
		map[string]interface{}{
			"authorized": true,
			"user_id":    userID,
			"permission": PermissionUserRead,
		},
	)
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: account-backend\internal\authorization\middleware.go
SIZE: 5263 bytes
====================================================================================================
package authorization

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"account-backend/internal/auth"
)

type authorizationContextKey string

const userIDContextKey authorizationContextKey = "authorization.user_id"

// ResourceIDResolver resolves the resource being accessed.
type ResourceIDResolver func(
	r *http.Request,
	userID string,
) *string

// ScopeIDResolver resolves the authorization scope.
type ScopeIDResolver func(
	r *http.Request,
	userID string,
) *string

// Middleware connects authenticated Auth sessions to Authorization.
type Middleware struct {
	sessionService       *auth.SessionService
	authorizationService *AuthorizationService
}

// NewMiddleware creates the Authorization middleware.
func NewMiddleware(
	sessionService *auth.SessionService,
	authorizationService *AuthorizationService,
) *Middleware {
	return &Middleware{
		sessionService:       sessionService,
		authorizationService: authorizationService,
	}
}

// Require applies a permission requirement to an HTTP handler.
func (m *Middleware) Require(
	permission string,
	resourceType string,
	resourceIDResolver ResourceIDResolver,
	scopeIDResolver ScopeIDResolver,
	next http.Handler,
) http.Handler {

	return http.HandlerFunc(
		func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {

			if m == nil ||
				m.sessionService == nil ||
				m.authorizationService == nil {

				writeAuthorizationError(
					w,
					http.StatusInternalServerError,
					"authorization middleware is not configured",
				)

				return
			}

			accessToken := extractBearerToken(
				r.Header.Get("Authorization"),
			)

			if accessToken == "" {
				writeAuthorizationError(
					w,
					http.StatusUnauthorized,
					"access token is required",
				)

				return
			}

			sessionResult, err :=
				m.sessionService.ValidateAccessToken(
					r.Context(),
					accessToken,
				)

			if err != nil {

				switch {
				case errors.Is(err, auth.ErrSessionNotFound),
					errors.Is(err, auth.ErrSessionRevoked),
					errors.Is(err, auth.ErrSessionExpired),
					errors.Is(err, auth.ErrEmptyAccessToken),
					errors.Is(err, auth.ErrInvalidToken):

					writeAuthorizationError(
						w,
						http.StatusUnauthorized,
						"invalid access token",
					)

				default:

					log.Printf(
						"authorization session validation error: %v",
						err,
					)

					writeAuthorizationError(
						w,
						http.StatusInternalServerError,
						"internal server error",
					)
				}

				return
			}

			if sessionResult == nil ||
				sessionResult.Session == nil ||
				!sessionResult.Valid {

				writeAuthorizationError(
					w,
					http.StatusUnauthorized,
					"invalid access token",
				)

				return
			}

			userID := sessionResult.Session.UserID

			var resourceID *string

			if resourceIDResolver != nil {
				resourceID = resourceIDResolver(
					r,
					userID,
				)
			}

			var scopeID *string

			if scopeIDResolver != nil {
				scopeID = scopeIDResolver(
					r,
					userID,
				)
			}

			result, err :=
				m.authorizationService.Authorize(
					r.Context(),
					AuthorizationRequest{
						UserID:       userID,
						Permission:   permission,
						ResourceType: resourceType,
						ResourceID:   resourceID,
						ScopeID:      scopeID,
					},
				)

			if err != nil {

				log.Printf(
					"authorization decision error: %v",
					err,
				)

				writeAuthorizationError(
					w,
					http.StatusInternalServerError,
					"internal server error",
				)

				return
			}

			if result.Decision != AuthorizationDecisionAllow {

				writeAuthorizationError(
					w,
					http.StatusForbidden,
					"access denied",
				)

				return
			}

			requestWithIdentity := r.WithContext(
				context.WithValue(
					r.Context(),
					userIDContextKey,
					userID,
				),
			)

			next.ServeHTTP(
				w,
				requestWithIdentity,
			)
		},
	)
}

// RequireSelf protects an endpoint for a user's own resource.
func (m *Middleware) RequireSelf(
	permission string,
	next http.Handler,
) http.Handler {

	return m.Require(
		permission,
		"user",
		func(
			_ *http.Request,
			userID string,
		) *string {

			return &userID
		},
		nil,
		next,
	)
}

// UserIDFromContext returns the authenticated user ID.
func UserIDFromContext(
	ctx context.Context,
) (string, bool) {

	value := ctx.Value(userIDContextKey)

	userID, ok := value.(string)

	return userID, ok
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Bearer token
// ------------------------------------------------------------

func extractBearerToken(
	authorization string,
) string {

	authorization = strings.TrimSpace(
		authorization,
	)

	if authorization == "" {
		return ""
	}

	const prefix = "Bearer "

	if !strings.HasPrefix(
		authorization,
		prefix,
	) {
		return ""
	}

	return strings.TrimSpace(
		strings.TrimPrefix(
			authorization,
			prefix,
		),
	)
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// JSON error
// ------------------------------------------------------------

func writeAuthorizationError(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	status int,
	message string,
) {

	w.Header().Set(
		"Content-Type",
		"application/json",
	)

	w.WriteHeader(status)

	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(
		map[string]string{
			"error": message,
		},
	)
}

FILE: account-backend\internal\authorization\middleware_test.go
SIZE: 6431 bytes

package authorization

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
	"account-backend/internal/auth"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
)

func TestAuthorizationMiddlewarePostgreSQL(
	t *testing.T,
) {

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(
		context.Background(),
		20*time.Second,
	)

	defer cancel()

	pool, err := pgxpool.New(
		ctx,
		"postgres://account:account_password@localhost:5432/account?sslmode=disable",
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to connect to PostgreSQL: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	defer pool.Close()

	if err := pool.Ping(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to ping PostgreSQL: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	db := postgres.NewClient(pool)

	sessionRepository := auth.NewSessionRepository(db)
	sessionService := auth.NewSessionService(
		sessionRepository,
	)

	authorizationRepository := NewRepository(db)

	authorizationService := NewAuthorizationService(
		authorizationRepository,
		authorizationRepository,
		authorizationRepository,
		authorizationRepository,
		authorizationRepository,
	)

	middleware := NewMiddleware(
		sessionService,
		authorizationService,
	)

	userID := createAuthorizationMiddlewareUser(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
	)

	defer cleanupAuthorizationMiddlewareUser(
		ctx,
		pool,
		userID,
	)

	role, err := authorizationRepository.GetRoleByCode(
		ctx,
		RoleUser,
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to load user role: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	assignAuthorizationMiddlewareRole(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
		userID,
		role.ID,
	)

	accessToken, _, _, err :=
		sessionRepository.Create(
			ctx,
			userID,
		)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to create test session: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	handler := middleware.RequireSelf(
		PermissionUserRead,
		http.HandlerFunc(
			func(
				w http.ResponseWriter,
				r *http.Request,
			) {

				contextUserID, ok :=
					UserIDFromContext(
						r.Context(),
					)

				if !ok ||
					contextUserID != userID {

					t.Errorf(
						"unexpected user id in context",
					)

					http.Error(
						w,
						"identity mismatch",
						http.StatusInternalServerError,
					)

					return
				}

				w.WriteHeader(
					http.StatusOK,
				)
			},
		),
	)

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Valid token + valid role = ALLOW
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	request := httptest.NewRequest(
		http.MethodGet,
		"/",
		nil,
	)

	request.Header.Set(
		"Authorization",
		"Bearer "+accessToken,
	)

	response := httptest.NewRecorder()

	handler.ServeHTTP(
		response,
		request,
	)

	if response.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected 200, got %d",
			response.Code,
		)
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Missing token = 401
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	request = httptest.NewRequest(
		http.MethodGet,
		"/",
		nil,
	)

	response = httptest.NewRecorder()

	handler.ServeHTTP(
		response,
		request,
	)

	if response.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected 401 for missing token, got %d",
			response.Code,
		)
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Revoke session = 401
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	sessionValidation, err :=
		sessionService.ValidateAccessToken(
			ctx,
			accessToken,
		)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to validate test session: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	if sessionValidation == nil ||
		sessionValidation.Session == nil ||
		sessionValidation.Session.ID == "" {

		t.Fatalf(
			"validated session is missing",
		)
	}

	if err := sessionService.RevokeSession(
		ctx,
		sessionValidation.Session.ID,
	); err != nil {

		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to revoke session: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	request = httptest.NewRequest(
		http.MethodGet,
		"/",
		nil,
	)

	request.Header.Set(
		"Authorization",
		"Bearer "+accessToken,
	)

	response = httptest.NewRecorder()

	handler.ServeHTTP(
		response,
		request,
	)

	if response.Code != http.StatusUnauthorized {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected 401 after session revocation, got %d",
			response.Code,
		)
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// User without role = 403
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	userWithoutRole := createAuthorizationMiddlewareUser(
		t,
		ctx,
		pool,
	)

	defer cleanupAuthorizationMiddlewareUser(
		ctx,
		pool,
		userWithoutRole,
	)

	accessToken2, _, _, err :=
		sessionRepository.Create(
			ctx,
			userWithoutRole,
		)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to create second test session: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	request = httptest.NewRequest(
		http.MethodGet,
		"/",
		nil,
	)

	request.Header.Set(
		"Authorization",
		"Bearer "+accessToken2,
	)

	response = httptest.NewRecorder()

	handler.ServeHTTP(
		response,
		request,
	)

	if response.Code != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf(
			"expected 403 for authenticated user without permission, got %d",
			response.Code,
		)
	}
}

func createAuthorizationMiddlewareUser(
	t *testing.T,
	ctx context.Context,
	pool *pgxpool.Pool,
) string {

	t.Helper()

	var userID string

	err := pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		INSERT INTO users (
			user_type
		)
		VALUES (
			'individual'
		)
		RETURNING id::text
		`,
	).Scan(&userID)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to create middleware test user: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	return userID
}

func assignAuthorizationMiddlewareRole(
	t *testing.T,
	ctx context.Context,
	pool *pgxpool.Pool,
	userID string,
	roleID string,
) {

	t.Helper()

	_, err := pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		INSERT INTO authorization_user_roles
		(
			user_id,
			role_id,
			starts_at,
			is_active
		)
		VALUES
		(
			$1::uuid,
			$2::uuid,
			NOW() - INTERVAL '1 minute',
			TRUE
		)
		`,
		userID,
		roleID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf(
			"failed to assign user role: %v",
			err,
		)
	}
}

func cleanupAuthorizationMiddlewareUser(
	ctx context.Context,
	pool *pgxpool.Pool,
	userID string,
) {

	_, _ = pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		DELETE FROM authorization_user_permission_overrides
		WHERE user_id = $1::uuid
		`,
		userID,
	)

	_, _ = pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		DELETE FROM authorization_user_roles
		WHERE user_id = $1::uuid
		`,
		userID,
	)

	_, _ = pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		DELETE FROM auth_sessions
		WHERE user_id = $1::uuid
		`,
		userID,
	)

	_, _ = pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		DELETE FROM users
		WHERE id = $1::uuid
		`,
		userID,
	)
}

package authorization

import "time"

// AccountType represents the type of user account.
// Account type is intentionally separate from Role.
type AccountType string

const (
	AccountTypeIndividual AccountType = "individual"
	AccountTypeLegal      AccountType = "legal"
)

// RoleType represents the category of a role.
type RoleType string

const (
	RoleTypeManagement  RoleType = "management"
	RoleTypeOperational RoleType = "operational"
	RoleTypeBase        RoleType = "base"
	RoleTypeSpecialized RoleType = "specialized"
	RoleTypeGeneral     RoleType = "general"
	RoleTypeSupervisory RoleType = "supervisory"
)

// ScopeType represents the authorization scope.
type ScopeType string

const (
	ScopeTypeGlobal       ScopeType = "global"
	ScopeTypeOrganization ScopeType = "organization"
	ScopeTypeProject      ScopeType = "project"
	ScopeTypeAsset        ScopeType = "asset"
	ScopeTypeContract     ScopeType = "contract"
	ScopeTypeSelf         ScopeType = "self"
	ScopeTypeCustom       ScopeType = "custom"
)

// PermissionEffect determines whether a permission is explicitly allowed or denied.
type PermissionEffect string

const (
	PermissionEffectAllow PermissionEffect = "allow"
	PermissionEffectDeny  PermissionEffect = "deny"
)

// AuthorizationDecision represents the final authorization decision.
type AuthorizationDecision string

const (
	AuthorizationDecisionAllow AuthorizationDecision = "allow"
	AuthorizationDecisionDeny  AuthorizationDecision = "deny"
)

// Role represents an authorization role.
type Role struct {
	ID               string
	Code             string
	Name             string
	RoleType         RoleType
	DefaultScopeType ScopeType
	Description      string
	IsSystemRole     bool
	IsActive         bool
	CreatedAt        time.Time
	UpdatedAt        time.Time
}

// Permission represents a resource + action permission.
type Permission struct {
	ID          string
	Code        string
	Resource    string
	Action      string
	Name        string
	Description string
	IsActive    bool
	CreatedAt   time.Time
	UpdatedAt   time.Time
}

// Scope represents an authorization scope.
type Scope struct {
	ID            string
	ScopeType     ScopeType
	ResourceType  *string
	ResourceID    *string
	ParentScopeID *string
	Name          string
	Description   string
	IsActive      bool
	CreatedAt     time.Time
	UpdatedAt     time.Time
}

// AuthorizationRequest represents an authorization check request.
type AuthorizationRequest struct {
	UserID       string
	Permission   string
	ResourceType string
	ResourceID   *string
	ScopeID      *string
}

// AuthorizationResult represents the result of an authorization decision.
type AuthorizationResult struct {
	Decision AuthorizationDecision
	Reason   string
}

// UserRole represents a role assigned to a user.
type UserRole struct {
	ID         string
	UserID     string
	RoleID     string
	ScopeID    *string
	AssignedBy *string
	StartsAt   time.Time
	ExpiresAt  *time.Time
	IsActive   bool
	CreatedAt  time.Time
	UpdatedAt  time.Time
}

// UserPermissionOverride represents an explicit allow/deny override.
type UserPermissionOverride struct {
	ID           string
	UserID       string
	PermissionID string
	Effect       PermissionEffect
	ScopeID      *string
	AssignedBy   *string
	StartsAt     time.Time
	ExpiresAt    *time.Time
	Reason       string
	IsActive     bool
	CreatedAt    time.Time
	UpdatedAt    time.Time
}

package authorization

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
)

// Repository provides PostgreSQL persistence for Authorization.
type Repository struct {
	db *postgres.Client
}

// NewRepository creates a new Authorization repository.
func NewRepository(db *postgres.Client) *Repository {
	return &Repository{
		db: db,
	}
}

func (r *Repository) validate() error {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New("authorization repository is nil")
	}

	return nil
}

// ============================================================
// Roles
// ============================================================

func (r *Repository) GetRoleByID(
	ctx context.Context,
	roleID string,
) (*Role, error) {

	if err := r.validate(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	query := `
		SELECT
			id::text,
			code,
			name,
			role_type,
			default_scope_type,
			COALESCE(description, ''),
			is_system_role,
			is_active,
			created_at,
			updated_at
		FROM authorization_roles
		WHERE id = $1
	`

	var role Role

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		query,
		roleID,
	).Scan(
		&role.ID,
		&role.Code,
		&role.Name,
		&role.RoleType,
		&role.DefaultScopeType,
		&role.Description,
		&role.IsSystemRole,
		&role.IsActive,
		&role.CreatedAt,
		&role.UpdatedAt,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrRoleNotFound, err)
	}

	return &role, nil
}

		&role.RoleType,

func (r *Repository) ListActiveRoles(
	ctx context.Context,
) ([]Role, error) {

	if err := r.validate(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	query := `
		SELECT
			id::text,
			code,
			name,
			role_type,
			default_scope_type,
			COALESCE(description, ''),
			is_system_role,
			is_active,
			created_at,
			updated_at
		FROM authorization_roles
		WHERE is_active = TRUE
		ORDER BY name
	`

	rows, err := r.db.Pool.Query(ctx, query)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()

	var roles []Role

	for rows.Next() {
		var role Role

		if err := rows.Scan(
			&role.ID,
			&role.Code,
			&role.Name,
			&role.RoleType,
			&role.DefaultScopeType,
			&role.Description,
			&role.IsSystemRole,
			&role.IsActive,
			&role.CreatedAt,
			&role.UpdatedAt,
		); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}

		roles = append(roles, role)
	}

	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return roles, nil
}

func (r *Repository) ListPermissions(
	ctx context.Context,
	roleID string,
) ([]Permission, error) {

	if err := r.validate(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	query := `
		SELECT
			p.id::text,
			p.code,
			p.resource,
			p.action,
			p.name,
			COALESCE(p.description, ''),
			p.is_active,
			p.created_at,
			p.updated_at
		FROM authorization_role_permissions rp
		INNER JOIN authorization_permissions p
			ON p.id = rp.permission_id
		WHERE rp.role_id = $1
		  AND p.is_active = TRUE
		ORDER BY p.resource, p.action
	`

	rows, err := r.db.Pool.Query(
		ctx,
		query,
		roleID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	defer rows.Close()

	var permissions []Permission

	for rows.Next() {
		var permission Permission

		if err := rows.Scan(
			&permission.ID,
			&permission.Code,
			&permission.Resource,
			&permission.Action,
			&permission.Name,
			&permission.Description,
			&permission.IsActive,
			&permission.CreatedAt,
			&permission.UpdatedAt,
		); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}

		permissions = append(permissions, permission)
	}

	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return permissions, nil
}

func (r *Repository) GetActiveUserRoles(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
) ([]UserRole, error) {

	if err := r.validate(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	query := `
		SELECT
			id::text,
			user_id::text,
			role_id::text,
			scope_id::text,
			assigned_by::text,
			starts_at,
			expires_at,
			is_active,
			created_at,
			updated_at
		FROM authorization_user_roles
		WHERE user_id = $1
		  AND is_active = TRUE
		  AND starts_at <= NOW()
		  AND (
			  expires_at IS NULL
			  OR expires_at > NOW()
		  )
	`

	rows, err := r.db.Pool.Query(
		ctx,
		query,
		userID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	defer rows.Close()

	var assignments []UserRole

	for rows.Next() {
		var assignment UserRole

		if err := rows.Scan(
			&assignment.ID,
			&assignment.UserID,
			&assignment.RoleID,
			&assignment.ScopeID,
			&assignment.AssignedBy,
			&assignment.StartsAt,
			&assignment.ExpiresAt,
			&assignment.IsActive,
			&assignment.CreatedAt,
			&assignment.UpdatedAt,
		); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}

		assignments = append(assignments, assignment)
	}

	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return assignments, nil
}

func (r *Repository) AssignRole(
	ctx context.Context,
	assignment UserRole,
) error {

	if err := r.validate(); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	query := `

			user_id,
			role_id,
			scope_id,
			assigned_by,
			starts_at,
			expires_at,
			is_active
		)
		VALUES
		(
			$1,
			$2,
			$3,
			$4,
			$5,
			$6,
			$7
		)
	`

	_, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		query,
		assignment.UserID,
		assignment.RoleID,
		assignment.ScopeID,
		assignment.AssignedBy,
		assignment.StartsAt,
		assignment.ExpiresAt,
		assignment.IsActive,
	)

	return err
}

			expires_at,
			COALESCE(reason, ''),

		  AND is_active = TRUE
		  AND starts_at <= NOW()

		  )

	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, err

	return overrides, nil
}

func (r *Repository) Update(
	ctx context.Context,
	scope Scope,
) error {

	if err := r.validate(); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	query := `
		UPDATE authorization_scopes
		SET
			scope_type = $1,
			resource_type = $2,
			resource_id = $3,
			parent_scope_id = $4,
			name = $5,
			description = $6,
			is_active = $7,
			updated_at = NOW()
		WHERE id = $8
	`

	_, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		query,
		scope.ScopeType,
		scope.ResourceType,
		scope.ResourceID,
		scope.ParentScopeID,
		scope.Name,
		scope.Description,
		scope.IsActive,
		scope.ID,
	)

	return err
}

func (r *Repository) Deactivate(
	ctx context.Context,
	scopeID string,
) error {

	if err := r.validate(); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	_, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE authorization_scopes
		SET
			is_active = FALSE,
			updated_at = NOW()
		WHERE id = $1
		`,
		scopeID,
	)

	return err
}

// IsScopeAllowed checks whether a requested scope is equal to
// or contained within the assigned scope.
//
// A nil assigned scope means unrestricted scope.
//
// A global assigned scope allows every request.
//
// Otherwise recursive parent relationships are followed.
func (r *Repository) IsScopeAllowed(
	ctx context.Context,
	assignedScopeID *string,
	requestedScopeID *string,
) (bool, error) {

	// No assigned scope means unrestricted access.
	// The authorization service controls where this is valid.
	if assignedScopeID == nil {
		return true, nil
	}

	// A scoped authorization request must contain a scope.
	if requestedScopeID == nil {
		return false, nil
	}

	if err := r.validate(); err != nil {
		return false, err
	}

	// A global assigned scope covers all requested scopes.
	var assignedScopeType ScopeType

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		SELECT scope_type
		FROM authorization_scopes
		WHERE id = $1
		  AND is_active = TRUE
		`,
		*assignedScopeID,
	).Scan(&assignedScopeType)

	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}

	if assignedScopeType == ScopeTypeGlobal {
		return true, nil
	}

	// Walk upward from the requested scope through its parents.
	//
	// Example:
	//
	// Ã˜Â³Ã˜Â§Ã˜Â²Ã™â€¦Ã˜Â§Ã™â€ 
	//   Ã¢â€â€Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Ã™Â¾Ã˜Â±Ã™Ë†ÃšËœÃ™â€¡
	//       Ã¢â€â€Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Ã˜Â¯Ã˜Â§Ã˜Â±Ã˜Â§Ã›Å’Ã›Å’
	//
	// An authorization assigned at the organization level
	// therefore covers its child project and asset scopes.
	query := `
		WITH RECURSIVE requested_scope_tree AS (
			SELECT
				id,
				parent_scope_id,
				is_active
			FROM authorization_scopes
			WHERE id = $1

			UNION ALL

			SELECT
				parent.id,
				parent.parent_scope_id,
				parent.is_active
			FROM authorization_scopes parent
			INNER JOIN requested_scope_tree child
				ON child.parent_scope_id = parent.id
		)
		SELECT EXISTS (
			SELECT 1
			FROM requested_scope_tree
			WHERE id = $2
			  AND is_active = TRUE
		)
	`

	var allowed bool

	err = r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		query,
		*requestedScopeID,
		*assignedScopeID,
	).Scan(&allowed)

	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}

	return allowed, nil
}

	_ PermissionRepository         = (*Repository)(nil)
	_ RoleAssignmentRepository     = (*Repository)(nil)
	_ PermissionOverrideRepository = (*Repository)(nil)
	_ ScopeRepository              = (*Repository)(nil)
)

package authorization

import "context"

// ScopeRepository defines persistence operations
// related to authorization scopes.
type ScopeRepository interface {
	GetByID(
		ctx context.Context,
		scopeID string,
	) (*Scope, error)

	Create(
		ctx context.Context,
		scope Scope,
	) error

	Update(
		ctx context.Context,
		scope Scope,
	) error

	Deactivate(
		ctx context.Context,
		scopeID string,
	) error

	IsScopeAllowed(
		ctx context.Context,
		assignedScopeID *string,
		requestedScopeID *string,
	) (bool, error)
}

// IsGlobal reports whether the scope applies to the whole system.
func (s Scope) IsGlobal() bool {
	return s.ScopeType == ScopeTypeGlobal
}

// IsSelf reports whether the scope applies to the user's own resources.
func (s Scope) IsSelf() bool {
	return s.ScopeType == ScopeTypeSelf
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: account-backend\internal\authorization\service.go
SIZE: 439 bytes
====================================================================================================
package authorization

// Service provides administrative authorization operations.
//
// This layer is intentionally separated from the final authorization
// decision engine. It will manage roles, permissions, assignments
// and scopes.
type Service struct {
	repository *Repository
}

// NewService creates a new Authorization service.
func NewService(repository *Repository) *Service {
	return &Service{
		repository: repository,
	}
}

    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),

    user_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,

    status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

        CHECK (user_type IN ('individual', 'legal')),

    CONSTRAINT users_status_check
        CHECK (status IN (
            'active',
            'limited',
            'suspended',
            'blocked',
            'closed'
        ))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS authorization_role_permissions (
    role_id UUID NOT NULL
        REFERENCES authorization_roles(id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    permission_id UUID NOT NULL
        REFERENCES authorization_permissions(id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    PRIMARY KEY (role_id, permission_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_role_permissions_permission
    ON authorization_role_permissions(permission_id);


-- ============================================================
-- Seed: User Role
-- ============================================================

INSERT INTO authorization_role_permissions (role_id, permission_id)
SELECT r.id, p.id
FROM authorization_roles r
CROSS JOIN authorization_permissions p
WHERE r.code = 'user'
  AND p.code IN (
      'user.read',
      'user.update',
      'profile.read',
      'profile.update',
      'bank_account.read',
      'bank_account.add',
      'bank_account.update',
      'market.read',
      'offer.read',
      'order.read',
      'order.create',
      'order.cancel',
      'contract.read',
      'contract.sign',
      'contractual_right.read',
      'contractual_right.purchase',
      'contractual_right.transfer',
      'wallet.read',
      'payment.read',
      'payment.create',
      'transaction.read',
      'verification.read',
      'verification.request'
  )
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

-- ============================================================
-- 017_create_authorization_user_roles.sql
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS authorization_user_roles (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),

    user_id UUID NOT NULL
        REFERENCES users(id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    role_id UUID NOT NULL
        REFERENCES authorization_roles(id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    scope_id UUID,

    assigned_by UUID
        REFERENCES users(id)
        ON DELETE SET NULL,

    starts_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    CONSTRAINT authorization_user_roles_dates_check
        CHECK (
            expires_at IS NULL
            OR expires_at > starts_at
        )
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_roles_user
    ON authorization_user_roles(user_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_roles_role
    ON authorization_user_roles(role_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_roles_scope
    ON authorization_user_roles(scope_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_roles_active
    ON authorization_user_roles(is_active);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_roles_expires
    ON authorization_user_roles(expires_at);

SIZE: 2127 bytes

-- ============================================================
-- 018_create_authorization_user_permission_overrides.sql
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS authorization_user_permission_overrides (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),

    user_id UUID NOT NULL
        REFERENCES users(id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    permission_id UUID NOT NULL
        REFERENCES authorization_permissions(id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    effect VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,

    scope_id UUID,

    assigned_by UUID
        REFERENCES users(id)
        ON DELETE SET NULL,

    starts_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    reason TEXT,

    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    CONSTRAINT authorization_user_permission_overrides_effect_check
        CHECK (
            effect IN (
                'allow',
                'deny'
            )
        ),

    CONSTRAINT authorization_user_permission_overrides_dates_check
        CHECK (
            expires_at IS NULL
            OR expires_at > starts_at
        )
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_permission_overrides_user
    ON authorization_user_permission_overrides(user_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_permission_overrides_permission
    ON authorization_user_permission_overrides(permission_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_permission_overrides_scope
    ON authorization_user_permission_overrides(scope_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_permission_overrides_effect
    ON authorization_user_permission_overrides(effect);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_permission_overrides_active
    ON authorization_user_permission_overrides(is_active);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_user_permission_overrides_expires
    ON authorization_user_permission_overrides(expires_at);

FILE: account-backend\migrations\019_create_authorization_scopes.sql

-- ============================================================
-- 019_create_authorization_scopes.sql
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS authorization_scopes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),

    scope_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    resource_type VARCHAR(100),

    resource_id UUID,

    parent_scope_id UUID
        REFERENCES authorization_scopes(id)
        ON DELETE CASCADE,

    name VARCHAR(200),

    description TEXT,

    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),

    CONSTRAINT authorization_scopes_scope_type_check
        CHECK (
            scope_type IN (
                'global',
                'organization',
                'project',
                'asset',
                'contract',
                'self',
                'custom'
            )
        ),

    CONSTRAINT authorization_scopes_resource_pair_check
        CHECK (
            (
                scope_type IN ('global', 'self')
                AND resource_type IS NULL
                AND resource_id IS NULL
            )
            OR
            (
                scope_type IN (
                    'organization',
                    'project',
                    'asset',
                    'contract',
                    'custom'
                )
            )
        )
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_scopes_scope_type
    ON authorization_scopes(scope_type);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_scopes_resource
    ON authorization_scopes(resource_type, resource_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_scopes_parent
    ON authorization_scopes(parent_scope_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_authorization_scopes_active
    ON authorization_scopes(is_active);


-- ============================================================
-- Foreign-key links to scopes
-- ============================================================

ALTER TABLE authorization_user_roles
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_authorization_user_roles_scope
    FOREIGN KEY (scope_id)
    REFERENCES authorization_scopes(id)
    ON DELETE SET NULL;

ALTER TABLE authorization_user_permission_overrides
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_authorization_user_permission_overrides_scope
    FOREIGN KEY (scope_id)
    REFERENCES authorization_scopes(id)
    ON DELETE SET NULL;


-- ============================================================
-- Global Scope
-- ============================================================

INSERT INTO authorization_scopes
(
    scope_type,
    name,
    description
)
SELECT
    'global',
    'Ú©Ù„ Ø³Ø§Ù…Ø§Ù†Ù‡',
    'Ù…Ø­Ø¯ÙˆØ¯Ù‡ Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„ Ø³Ø§Ù…Ø§Ù†Ù‡'
WHERE NOT EXISTS (

    FROM authorization_scopes

);

package main

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"account-backend/config"
	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
	redisinfra "account-backend/infrastructure/redis"
	"account-backend/internal/auth"

	"account-backend/internal/authorization"
	"account-backend/internal/user"
	"account-backend/internal/verification"
)

type Application struct {
	Config   config.Config
	Postgres *postgres.Client
	Redis    *redisinfra.Client
}

func main() {
	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("configuration error: %v", err)
	}

	ctx := context.Background()

	// PostgreSQL
	postgresPool, err := postgres.NewPool(
		ctx,
		cfg.PostgreSQL,
	)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf(
			"PostgreSQL initialization error: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	postgresClient := postgres.NewClient(postgresPool)

	// Redis
	redisClient, err := redisinfra.NewClient(
		ctx,
		cfg.Redis,
	)
	if err != nil {
		postgresClient.Close()

		log.Fatalf(
			"Redis initialization error: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	redisWrapper := redisinfra.NewClientWrapper(
		redisClient,
	)

	app := &Application{
		Config:   cfg,
		Postgres: postgresClient,
		Redis:    redisWrapper,
	}

	// =========================================================
	// User Module
	// =========================================================

	userRepository := user.NewRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	userService := user.NewService(
		userRepository,
	)

	userHandler := user.NewHandler(
		userService,
	)

	// =========================================================
	// User Profile Module
	// =========================================================

	profileRepository := user.NewProfileRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	profileService := user.NewProfileService(
		profileRepository,
	)

	profileHandler := user.NewProfileHandler(
		profileService,
	)

	// =========================================================
	// Legal Entity Module
	// =========================================================

	legalEntityRepository := user.NewLegalEntityRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	legalEntityService := user.NewLegalEntityService(
		legalEntityRepository,
	)

	legalEntityHandler := user.NewLegalEntityHandler(
		legalEntityService,
	)

	// =========================================================
	// User Phone Module
	// =========================================================

	phoneRepository := user.NewPhoneRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	phoneService := user.NewPhoneService(
		phoneRepository,
	)

	phoneHandler := user.NewPhoneHandler(
		phoneService,
	)
	// =========================================================
	// =========================================================
	// User Email Module
	// =========================================================

	emailRepository := user.NewEmailRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	emailService := user.NewEmailService(
		emailRepository,
	)

	emailHandler := user.NewEmailHandler(
		emailService,
	)
	// =========================================================
	// Auth Module
	// =========================================================

	authRepository := auth.NewRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	authService := auth.NewService(
		authRepository,
	)

	// Router
	// =========================================================
	// =========================================================
	// Verification Module
	// =========================================================

	verificationRepository := verification.NewRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	verificationAuditRepository := verification.NewAuditRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	verificationProviders := verification.NewProviderManager()
	testProvider := verification.NewTestProvider()

	if err := verificationProviders.Register(
		testProvider,
	); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf(
			"Verification test provider registration error: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	verificationCache := verification.NewCache(
		redisWrapper,
	)

	verificationProtection := verification.NewProtection(
		verificationCache,
	)

	verificationService := verification.NewService(
		verificationRepository,
		verificationProviders,
		verificationCache,
	)

	// =========================================================
	// Auth Login
	// =========================================================

	loginRepository := auth.NewLoginRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	trustedDeviceRepository := auth.NewTrustedDeviceRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	sessionRepository := auth.NewSessionRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	sessionService := auth.NewSessionService(
		sessionRepository,
	)

	// =========================================================
	// Authorization Module
	// =========================================================

	authorizationRepository := authorization.NewRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	authorizationService := authorization.NewAuthorizationService(
		authorizationRepository,
		authorizationRepository,
		authorizationRepository,
		authorizationRepository,
		authorizationRepository,
	)

	authorizationMiddleware := authorization.NewMiddleware(
		sessionService,
		authorizationService,
	)

	authorizationHandler := authorization.NewHandler()

	sessionHandler := auth.NewSessionHandler(
		sessionService,
	)

	passwordRepository := auth.NewPasswordRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	passwordService := auth.NewPasswordService(
		passwordRepository,
		sessionService,
	)

	passwordHandler := auth.NewPasswordHandler(
		passwordService,
	)

	passwordResetRepository := auth.NewPasswordResetRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	passwordResetService := auth.NewPasswordResetService(
		passwordResetRepository,
		passwordRepository,
		sessionService,
		trustedDeviceRepository,
		verificationService,
		"test-provider",
	)

	passwordResetHandler := auth.NewPasswordResetHandler(
		passwordResetService,
	)

	trustedDeviceManagementService := auth.NewTrustedDeviceManagementService(
		trustedDeviceRepository,
		sessionService,
	)

	trustedDeviceManagementHandler := auth.NewTrustedDeviceManagementHandler(
		trustedDeviceManagementService,
	)

	authAuditRepository := auth.NewAuthAuditRepository(
		postgresClient,
	)

	loginService := auth.NewLoginService(
		loginRepository,
		verificationService,
		trustedDeviceRepository,
		sessionRepository,
		authAuditRepository,
		"test-provider",
	)

	authHandler := auth.NewHandler(
		authService,
		loginService,
	)

	verificationHandler := verification.NewHandler(
		verificationService,
		verificationProtection,
		verificationAuditRepository,
	)

	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/register",
		authHandler.Register,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/login",
		authHandler.Login,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/refresh",
		sessionHandler.Refresh,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/logout",
		sessionHandler.Logout,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/logout-all",
		sessionHandler.LogoutAllDevices,
	)

	mux.Handle(
		"/api/v1/auth/authorization/me",
		authorizationMiddleware.RequireSelf(
			authorization.PermissionUserRead,
			http.HandlerFunc(
				authorizationHandler.Me,
			),
		),
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/devices",
		trustedDeviceManagementHandler.List,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/devices/revoke-all",
		trustedDeviceManagementHandler.RevokeAll,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/devices/",
		trustedDeviceManagementHandler.HandleDevice,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/password/change",
		passwordHandler.ChangePassword,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/password/forgot",
		passwordResetHandler.ForgotPassword,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/auth/password/reset",
		passwordResetHandler.ResetPassword,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/health",
		app.healthHandler,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/ready",
		app.readyHandler,
	)

	// Create User
	// Verification
	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/verifications",
		verificationHandler.CreateVerification,
	)

	// Execute Verification
	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/verifications/execute",
		verificationHandler.ExecuteVerification,
	)

	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/verifications/",
		verificationHandler.GetVerification,
	)
	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/users",
		userHandler.CreateUser,
	)

	// Create User Profile
	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/users/profile",
		profileHandler.CreateProfile,
	)

	// Create User Phone
	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/users/phone",
		phoneHandler.CreatePhone,
	)

	// Create User Email
	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/users/email",
		emailHandler.CreateEmail,
	)
	// Create Legal Entity
	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/users/legal-entity",
		legalEntityHandler.CreateLegalEntity,
	)
	// User and User Profile
	mux.HandleFunc(
		"/api/v1/users/",
		func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {

			path := strings.TrimSuffix(
				r.URL.Path,
				"/",
			)

			if strings.HasSuffix(
				path,
				"/profile",
			) {
				profileHandler.GetProfileByUserID(
					w,
					r,
				)
				return
			}

			if strings.HasSuffix(
				path,
				"/legal-entity",
			) {
				legalEntityHandler.GetLegalEntityByUserID(
					w,
					r,
				)
				return
			}

			if strings.HasSuffix(
				path,
				"/phones",
			) {
				phoneHandler.GetPhonesByUserID(
					w,
					r,
				)
				return
			}

			if strings.HasSuffix(
				path,
				"/emails",
			) {
				emailHandler.GetEmailsByUserID(
					w,
					r,
				)
				return
			}
			userHandler.GetUserByID(
				w,
				r,
			)
		},
	)

	// =========================================================
	// HTTP Server
	// =========================================================

	server := &http.Server{
		Addr: cfg.Server.Host + ":" + strconv.Itoa(
			cfg.Server.Port,
		),

		Handler: mux,

		ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second,
		ReadTimeout:       15 * time.Second,
		WriteTimeout:      15 * time.Second,
		IdleTimeout:       60 * time.Second,
	}

	go func() {
		log.Printf(
			"Account Backend started on %s",
			server.Addr,
		)

		if err := server.ListenAndServe(); err != nil &&
			!errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {

			log.Fatalf(
				"HTTP server error: %v",
				err,
			)
		}
	}()

	// =========================================================
	// Graceful Shutdown
	// =========================================================

	stop := make(chan os.Signal, 1)

	signal.Notify(
		stop,
		os.Interrupt,
		syscall.SIGTERM,
	)

	<-stop

	log.Println("Shutdown signal received")

	shutdownContext, cancel := context.WithTimeout(
		context.Background(),
		10*time.Second,
	)
	defer cancel()

	if err := server.Shutdown(shutdownContext); err != nil {
		log.Printf(
			"HTTP server shutdown error: %v",
			err,
		)
	}

	redisWrapper.Close()
	postgresClient.Close()

	log.Println("Account Backend stopped")
}

// =============================================================
// Health
// =============================================================

func (app *Application) healthHandler(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		map[string]string{
			"status":  "ok",
			"service": "account-backend",
		},
	)
}

// =============================================================
// Ready
// =============================================================

func (app *Application) readyHandler(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	checkContext, cancel := context.WithTimeout(
		r.Context(),
		2*time.Second,
	)
	defer cancel()

	postgresStatus := "ok"
	redisStatus := "ok"

	if err := app.Postgres.Ping(checkContext); err != nil {
		postgresStatus = "error"
	}

	if err := app.Redis.Ping(checkContext); err != nil {
		redisStatus = "error"
	}

	if postgresStatus != "ok" ||
		redisStatus != "ok" {

		writeJSON(
			w,
			http.StatusServiceUnavailable,
			map[string]interface{}{
				"status":     "not_ready",
				"postgresql": postgresStatus,
				"redis":      redisStatus,
			},
		)

		return
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		map[string]interface{}{
			"status":     "ready",
			"postgresql": postgresStatus,
			"redis":      redisStatus,
		},
	)
}

// =============================================================
// JSON Response
// =============================================================

func writeJSON(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	status int,
	data interface{},
) {
	w.Header().Set(
		"Content-Type",
		"application/json",
	)

	w.WriteHeader(status)

	if err := json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(data); err != nil {
		log.Printf(
			"failed to write JSON response: %v",
			err,
		)
	}
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"time"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
)

type AuthAudit struct {
	ID          string
	UserID      string
	Operation   string
	Status      string
	IPAddress   string
	UserAgent   string
	ErrorCode   string
	Metadata    []byte
	CreatedAt   time.Time
}

type AuthAuditRepository struct {
	db *postgres.Client
}

func NewAuthAuditRepository(
	db *postgres.Client,
) *AuthAuditRepository {
	return &AuthAuditRepository{
		db: db,
	}
}

func (r *AuthAuditRepository) Create(
	ctx context.Context,
	audit *AuthAudit,
) error {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New("auth audit repository is nil")
	}

	if audit == nil {
		return errors.New("auth audit is nil")
	}

	_, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		INSERT INTO auth_audits (
			user_id,
			operation,
			status,
			ip_address,
			user_agent,
			error_code,
			metadata
		)
		VALUES (
			NULLIF($1, '')::uuid,
			$2,
			$3,
			NULLIF($4, '')::inet,
			$5,
			NULLIF($6, ''),
			$7
		)
		`,
		audit.UserID,
		audit.Operation,
		audit.Status,
		audit.IPAddress,
		audit.UserAgent,
		audit.ErrorCode,
		audit.Metadata,
	)

	return err
}

func auditMetadata(values map[string]string) []byte {

	if len(values) == 0 {
		return []byte(`{}`)
	}

	data, err := json.Marshal(values)

	if err != nil {
		return []byte(`{}`)
	}

	return data
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: account-backend\internal\auth\handler.go
SIZE: 3623 bytes

package auth

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log"
	"net/http"
)

type Handler struct {
	service      *Service
	loginService *LoginService
}

func NewHandler(
	service *Service,
	loginService *LoginService,
) *Handler {
	return &Handler{
		service:      service,
		loginService: loginService,
	}
}

func (h *Handler) Register(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {

	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)
		return
	}

	if h == nil || h.service == nil {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusInternalServerError,
			"auth service is unavailable",
		)
		return
	}

	var request RegistrationRequest

	if err := json.NewDecoder(
		r.Body,
	).Decode(&request); err != nil {

		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusBadRequest,
			"invalid request body",
		)
		return
	}

	result, err := h.service.Register(
		r.Context(),
		&request,
	)

	if err != nil {

		switch {

		case errors.Is(
			err,
			ErrNationalIDAlreadyRegistered,
		):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusConflict,
				err.Error(),
			)

		case errors.Is(
			err,
			ErrPhoneAlreadyRegistered,
		):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusConflict,
				err.Error(),
			)

		case errors.Is(
			err,
			ErrEmailAlreadyRegistered,
		):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusConflict,
				err.Error(),
			)

		default:

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusBadRequest,
				err.Error(),
			)
		}

		return
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusCreated,
		result,
	)
}

func (h *Handler) Login(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {

	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {

		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)

		return
	}

	if h == nil || h.loginService == nil {

		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusInternalServerError,
			"login service is unavailable",
		)

		return
	}

	var request LoginRequest

	if err := json.NewDecoder(
		r.Body,
	).Decode(&request); err != nil {

		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusBadRequest,
			"invalid request body",
		)

		return
	}

	result, err := h.loginService.Login(
		r.Context(),
		&request,
		r.RemoteAddr,
		r.UserAgent(),
	)

	if err != nil {

		switch {

		case errors.Is(
			err,
			ErrInvalidCredentials,
		):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				err.Error(),
			)

		case errors.Is(
			err,
			ErrAccountLocked,
		):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusLocked,
				err.Error(),
			)

		case errors.Is(
			err,
			ErrAccountInactive,
		):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusForbidden,
				err.Error(),
			)

		case errors.Is(
			err,
			ErrVerificationFailed,
		):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				err.Error(),
			)

		default:
			log.Printf(
				"auth login internal error: %v",
				err,
			)

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusInternalServerError,
				err.Error(),
			)
		}

		return
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		result,
	)
}

func writeJSON(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	statusCode int,
	data any,
) {

	w.Header().Set(
		"Content-Type",
		"application/json",
	)

	w.WriteHeader(statusCode)

	_ = json.NewEncoder(
		w,
	).Encode(data)
}

func writeJSONError(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	statusCode int,
	message string,
) {

	writeJSON(
		w,
		statusCode,
		map[string]string{
			"error": message,
		},
	)
}

var (
	ErrInvalidCredentials = errors.New(
		"invalid credentials",

	ErrAccountLocked = errors.New(
		"account is temporarily locked",
	)

	ErrAccountInactive = errors.New(
		"account is not active",

		"verification failed",
	)
