package auth

import "time"

type LoginRequest struct {
	NationalID           string `json:"national_id"`
	Password             string `json:"password"`
	DeviceToken          string `json:"device_token,omitempty"`
	TrustThisDevice      bool   `json:"trust_this_device,omitempty"`
	DeviceName           string `json:"device_name,omitempty"`
	VerificationProvider string `json:"verification_provider,omitempty"`
	VerificationCode     string `json:"verification_code,omitempty"`
}

type LoginResponse struct {
	UserID               string `json:"user_id"`
	Status               string `json:"status"`
	Authenticated        bool   `json:"authenticated"`
	VerificationRequired bool   `json:"verification_required"`
	VerificationStatus   string `json:"verification_status,omitempty"`
	AccessToken          string `json:"access_token,omitempty"`
	RefreshToken         string `json:"refresh_token,omitempty"`
	TrustedDeviceToken   string `json:"trusted_device_token,omitempty"`
	ExpiresIn            int64  `json:"expires_in,omitempty"`
}

type loginCredentials struct {
	UserID              string
	UserStatus          string
	PasswordHash        string
	FailedLoginAttempts int
	LockedUntil         *time.Time
	PhoneNumber         string
	PhoneVerified       bool
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: account-backend\internal\auth\login_repository.go
SIZE: 3408 bytes
====================================================================================================
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
)

type LoginRepository struct {
	db *postgres.Client
}

func NewLoginRepository(
	db *postgres.Client,
) *LoginRepository {
	return &LoginRepository{
		db: db,
	}
}

func (r *LoginRepository) GetLoginCredentials(
	ctx context.Context,
	nationalID string,
) (*loginCredentials, error) {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("login repository is nil")
	}

	query := `
		SELECT
			u.id,
			u.status,
			ac.password_hash,
			ac.failed_login_attempts,
			ac.locked_until,
			COALESCE(up.phone_number, ''),
			COALESCE(up.is_verified, FALSE)
		FROM users u
		INNER JOIN user_profiles p
			ON p.user_id = u.id
		INNER JOIN auth_credentials ac
			ON ac.user_id = u.id
		LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
			SELECT
				phone_number,
				is_verified
			FROM user_phones
			WHERE user_id = u.id
			  AND is_primary = TRUE
			ORDER BY created_at ASC
			LIMIT 1
		) up ON TRUE
		WHERE p.national_id = $1
	`

	var result loginCredentials

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		query,
		nationalID,
	).Scan(
		&result.UserID,
		&result.UserStatus,
		&result.PasswordHash,
		&result.FailedLoginAttempts,
		&result.LockedUntil,
		&result.PhoneNumber,
		&result.PhoneVerified,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
			return nil, ErrInvalidCredentials
		}

		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to get login credentials: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return &result, nil
}

func (r *LoginRepository) RegisterFailedAttempt(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
	lockUntil *time.Time,
) error {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New("login repository is nil")
	}

	_, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE auth_credentials
		SET
			failed_login_attempts =
				failed_login_attempts + 1,
			locked_until = $2,
			updated_at = NOW()
		WHERE user_id = $1
		`,
		userID,
		lockUntil,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to register failed login attempt: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return nil
}

func (r *LoginRepository) ResetFailedAttempts(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
) error {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New("login repository is nil")
	}

	_, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE auth_credentials
		SET
			failed_login_attempts = 0,
			locked_until = NULL,
			updated_at = NOW()
		WHERE user_id = $1
		`,
		userID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to reset failed login attempts: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return nil
}

func (r *LoginRepository) MarkPrimaryPhoneVerified(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
) error {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New("login repository is nil")
	}

	_, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE user_phones
		SET
			is_verified = TRUE,
			verified_at = NOW(),
			updated_at = NOW()
		WHERE user_id = $1
		  AND is_primary = TRUE
		`,
		userID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to mark primary phone as verified: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return nil
}

func normalizeLoginNationalID(value string) string {
	return strings.TrimSpace(value)
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: account-backend\internal\auth\login_service.go
SIZE: 8793 bytes
====================================================================================================
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"account-backend/internal/verification"

	"golang.org/x/crypto/bcrypt"
)

const (
	maxFailedLoginAttempts = 5
	loginLockDuration      = 15 * time.Minute
)

type LoginService struct {
	repository             *LoginRepository
	verificationService    *verification.Service
	trustedDevices         *TrustedDeviceRepository
	sessions               *SessionRepository
	audit                  *AuthAuditRepository
	defaultProvider        string
}

func NewLoginService(
	repository *LoginRepository,
	verificationService *verification.Service,
	trustedDevices *TrustedDeviceRepository,
	sessions *SessionRepository,
	audit *AuthAuditRepository,
	defaultProvider string,
) *LoginService {

	defaultProvider = strings.TrimSpace(defaultProvider)

	return &LoginService{
		repository:          repository,
		verificationService: verificationService,
		trustedDevices:      trustedDevices,
		sessions:            sessions,
		audit:               audit,
		defaultProvider:     defaultProvider,
	}
}

func normalizeRemoteIP(remoteAddr string) string {

	if remoteAddr == "" {
		return ""
	}

	host, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(remoteAddr)

	if err == nil {
		return host
	}

	return remoteAddr
}

func (s *LoginService) writeAudit(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
	operation string,
	status string,
	ipAddress string,
	userAgent string,
	errorCode string,
	metadata map[string]string,
) {

	if s == nil || s.audit == nil {
		return
	}

	audit := &AuthAudit{
		UserID:    userID,
		Operation: operation,
		Status:    status,
		IPAddress: normalizeRemoteIP(ipAddress),
		UserAgent: userAgent,
		ErrorCode: errorCode,
		Metadata:  auditMetadata(metadata),
	}

	_ = s.audit.Create(ctx, audit)
}

func (s *LoginService) Login(
	ctx context.Context,
	request *LoginRequest,
	remoteAddr string,
	userAgent string,
) (*LoginResponse, error) {

	if s == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("login service is nil")
	}

	if s.repository == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("login repository is nil")
	}

	if request == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("login request is required")
	}

	request.NationalID = normalizeLoginNationalID(
		request.NationalID,
	)

	request.Password = strings.TrimSpace(
		request.Password,
	)

	request.DeviceToken = strings.TrimSpace(
		request.DeviceToken,
	)

	request.DeviceName = strings.TrimSpace(
		request.DeviceName,
	)

	request.VerificationProvider = strings.TrimSpace(
		request.VerificationProvider,
	)

	if request.NationalID == "" {
		return nil, ErrInvalidCredentials
	}

	if request.Password == "" {
		return nil, ErrInvalidCredentials
	}

	credentials, err := s.repository.GetLoginCredentials(
		ctx,
		request.NationalID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		s.writeAudit(
			ctx,
			"",
			"login",
			"failed",
			remoteAddr,
			userAgent,
			"invalid_credentials",
			nil,
		)

		return nil, ErrInvalidCredentials
	}

	if credentials.LockedUntil != nil &&
		time.Now().Before(*credentials.LockedUntil) {

		s.writeAudit(
			ctx,
			credentials.UserID,
			"login",
			"failed",
			remoteAddr,
			userAgent,
			"account_locked",
			nil,
		)

		return nil, ErrAccountLocked
	}

	switch credentials.UserStatus {
	case "active":
		// allowed

	default:

		s.writeAudit(
			ctx,
			credentials.UserID,
			"login",
			"failed",
			remoteAddr,
			userAgent,
			"account_inactive",
			map[string]string{
				"user_status": credentials.UserStatus,
			},
		)

		return nil, ErrAccountInactive
	}

	err = bcrypt.CompareHashAndPassword(
		[]byte(credentials.PasswordHash),
		[]byte(request.Password),
	)

	if err != nil {

		attempts := credentials.FailedLoginAttempts + 1

		var lockUntil *time.Time

		if attempts >= maxFailedLoginAttempts {
			value := time.Now().Add(loginLockDuration)
			lockUntil = &value
		}

		_ = s.repository.RegisterFailedAttempt(
			ctx,
			credentials.UserID,
			lockUntil,
		)

		if lockUntil != nil {

			s.writeAudit(
				ctx,
				credentials.UserID,
				"login",
				"failed",
				remoteAddr,
				userAgent,
				"account_locked",
				map[string]string{
					"attempts": "5",
				},
			)

			return nil, ErrAccountLocked
		}

		s.writeAudit(
			ctx,
			credentials.UserID,
			"login",
			"failed",
			remoteAddr,
			userAgent,
			"invalid_password",
			nil,
		)

		return nil, ErrInvalidCredentials
	}

	if err := s.repository.ResetFailedAttempts(
		ctx,
		credentials.UserID,
	); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Trusted Device Check
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	trustedDevice := false

	if s.trustedDevices != nil &&
		request.DeviceToken != "" {

		trustedDevice, err =
			s.trustedDevices.FindValid(
				ctx,
				credentials.UserID,
				request.DeviceToken,
			)

		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}

	verificationRequired := !trustedDevice

	var verificationStatus string

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Verification
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	if verificationRequired {

		if s.verificationService == nil {
			return nil, errors.New(
				"verification service is unavailable",
			)
		}

		provider := request.VerificationProvider

		if provider == "" {
			provider = s.defaultProvider
		}

		if provider == "" {
			return nil, errors.New(
				"verification provider is not configured",
			)
		}

		data := map[string]string{
			"contact_type":  "mobile",
			"contact_value": credentials.PhoneNumber,
		}

		if request.VerificationCode != "" {
			data["verification_code"] =
				request.VerificationCode
		}

		result, err :=
			s.verificationService.ExecuteVerification(
				ctx,
				credentials.UserID,
				verification.VerificationTypeContact,
				provider,
				data,
			)

		if err != nil {

			s.writeAudit(
				ctx,
				credentials.UserID,
				"login",
				"failed",
				remoteAddr,
				userAgent,
				"verification_failed",
				map[string]string{
					"provider": provider,
				},
			)

			return nil, ErrVerificationFailed
		}

		if result == nil ||
			result.Status != verification.VerificationStatusVerified {

			s.writeAudit(
				ctx,
				credentials.UserID,
				"login",
				"failed",
				remoteAddr,
				userAgent,
				"verification_not_verified",
				map[string]string{
					"provider": provider,
				},
			)

			return nil, ErrVerificationFailed
		}

		verificationStatus =
			string(result.Status)

		// A successful contact verification confirms
		// the primary phone account state.
		if err := s.repository.MarkPrimaryPhoneVerified(
			ctx,
			credentials.UserID,
		); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Session
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	if s.sessions == nil {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"session repository is unavailable",
		)
	}

	accessToken, refreshToken, expiresAt, err :=
		s.sessions.Create(
			ctx,
			credentials.UserID,
		)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	response := &LoginResponse{
		UserID:               credentials.UserID,
		Status:               "authenticated",
		Authenticated:        true,
		VerificationRequired: verificationRequired,
		VerificationStatus:   verificationStatus,
		AccessToken:          accessToken,
		RefreshToken:         refreshToken,
		ExpiresIn: int64(
			time.Until(expiresAt).Seconds(),
		),
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Trust This Device
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	if verificationRequired &&
		request.TrustThisDevice &&
		s.trustedDevices != nil {

		token, err :=
			s.trustedDevices.Create(
				ctx,
				credentials.UserID,
				request.DeviceName,
			)

		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}

		response.TrustedDeviceToken = token
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Audit
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	auditStatus := "success"

	if trustedDevice {
		auditStatus = "trusted_device"
	}

	s.writeAudit(
		ctx,
		credentials.UserID,
		"login",
		auditStatus,
		remoteAddr,
		userAgent,
		"",
		map[string]string{
			"verification_required": boolToString(
				verificationRequired,
			),
		},
	)

	return response, nil
}

func boolToString(value bool) string {

	if value {
		return "true"
	}

	return "false"
}

package auth

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log"
	"net/http"
)

type PasswordHandler struct {
	service *PasswordService
}

func NewPasswordHandler(
	service *PasswordService,
) *PasswordHandler {
	return &PasswordHandler{
		service: service,
	}
}

func (h *PasswordHandler) ChangePassword(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)
		return
	}

	accessToken := extractBearerToken(
		r.Header.Get("Authorization"),
	)

	if accessToken == "" {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusUnauthorized,
			"access token is required",
		)
		return
	}

	var request ChangePasswordRequest

	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&request); err != nil {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusBadRequest,
			"invalid request body",
		)
		return
	}

	err := h.service.ChangePassword(
		r.Context(),
		accessToken,
		request.CurrentPassword,
		request.NewPassword,
	)

	if err != nil {
		switch {
		case errors.Is(err, ErrCurrentPasswordRequired),
			errors.Is(err, ErrNewPasswordRequired),
			errors.Is(err, ErrNewPasswordTooShort),
			errors.Is(err, ErrNewPasswordSameAsCurrent):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusBadRequest,
				err.Error(),
			)

		case errors.Is(err, ErrCurrentPasswordIncorrect):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				"current password is incorrect",
			)

		case errors.Is(err, ErrSessionNotFound),
			errors.Is(err, ErrSessionRevoked),
			errors.Is(err, ErrSessionExpired),
			errors.Is(err, ErrEmptyAccessToken):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				"invalid access token",
			)

		default:

			log.Printf(
				"auth password change internal error: %v",
				err,
			)

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusInternalServerError,
				"internal server error",
			)
		}

		return
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		ChangePasswordResponse{
			Message: "password changed successfully",
		},
	)
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
)

type PasswordRepository struct {
	db *postgres.Client
}

func NewPasswordRepository(
	db *postgres.Client,
) *PasswordRepository {
	return &PasswordRepository{
		db: db,
	}
}

func (r *PasswordRepository) GetPasswordHash(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
) (string, error) {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return "", errors.New("password repository is nil")
	}

	if userID == "" {
		return "", errors.New("user id is empty")
	}

	var passwordHash string

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		SELECT password_hash
		FROM auth_credentials
		WHERE user_id = $1
		`,
		userID,
	).Scan(&passwordHash)

	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to get password hash: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return passwordHash, nil
}

func (r *PasswordRepository) UpdatePassword(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
	passwordHash string,
) error {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New("password repository is nil")
	}

	if userID == "" {
		return errors.New("user id is empty")
	}

	if passwordHash == "" {
		return errors.New("password hash is empty")
	}

	now := time.Now()

	commandTag, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE auth_credentials
		SET
			password_hash = $2,
			password_changed_at = $3,
			failed_login_attempts = 0,
			locked_until = NULL,
			updated_at = $3
		WHERE user_id = $1
		`,
		userID,
		passwordHash,
		now,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to update password: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if commandTag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
		return errors.New(
			"auth credentials not found",
		)
	}

	return nil
}

package auth

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"time"
)

type PasswordResetHandler struct {
	service *PasswordResetService
}

func NewPasswordResetHandler(
	service *PasswordResetService,
) *PasswordResetHandler {
	return &PasswordResetHandler{
		service: service,
	}
}

func (h *PasswordResetHandler) ForgotPassword(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)
		return
	}

	var request ForgotPasswordRequest

	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&request); err != nil {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusBadRequest,
			"invalid request body",
		)
		return
	}

	token, expiresAt, err := h.service.ForgotPassword(
		r.Context(),
		request.NationalID,
		request.VerificationProvider,
		request.VerificationCode,
	)

	if err != nil {
		switch {
		case errors.Is(err, ErrResetAccountNotFound),
			errors.Is(err, ErrResetVerificationFailed):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				"password reset verification failed",
			)

		default:

			log.Printf(
				"auth forgot password internal error: %v",
				err,
			)

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusInternalServerError,
				"internal server error",
			)
		}

		return
	}

	expiresIn := int64(
		time.Until(expiresAt).Seconds(),
	)

	if expiresIn < 0 {
		expiresIn = 0
	}

	// The reset token is returned here because the current
	// environment uses test-provider. A real SMS provider
	// can later deliver the token through the actual channel.
	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		ForgotPasswordResponse{
			Message:    "password reset verification successful",
			ResetToken: token,
			ExpiresIn:  expiresIn,
		},
	)
}

func (h *PasswordResetHandler) ResetPassword(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)
		return
	}

	var request ResetPasswordRequest

	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&request); err != nil {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusBadRequest,
			"invalid request body",
		)
		return
	}

	err := h.service.ResetPassword(
		r.Context(),
		request.ResetToken,
		request.NewPassword,
	)

	if err != nil {
		switch {
		case errors.Is(err, ErrResetNewPasswordRequired),
			errors.Is(err, ErrResetNewPasswordTooShort):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusBadRequest,
				err.Error(),
			)

		case errors.Is(err, ErrResetTokenInvalid),
			errors.Is(err, ErrResetTokenExpired),
			errors.Is(err, ErrResetTokenUsed):

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				"invalid or expired password reset token",
			)

		default:

			log.Printf(
				"auth reset password internal error: %v",
				err,
			)

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusInternalServerError,
				"internal server error",
			)
		}

		return
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		ResetPasswordResponse{
			Message: "password reset successfully",
		},
	)
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
)

const passwordResetTokenTTL = 10 * time.Minute

type PasswordResetRepository struct {
	db *postgres.Client
}

type PasswordResetAccount struct {
	UserID      string
	PhoneNumber string
}

type PasswordResetToken struct {
	ID        string
	UserID    string
	ExpiresAt time.Time
	UsedAt    *time.Time
}

func NewPasswordResetRepository(
	db *postgres.Client,
) *PasswordResetRepository {
	return &PasswordResetRepository{
		db: db,
	}
}

func generatePasswordResetToken() (string, error) {
	buffer := make([]byte, 32)

	if _, err := rand.Read(buffer); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(buffer), nil
}

func hashPasswordResetToken(token string) string {
	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(token))

	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(hash[:])
}

func (r *PasswordResetRepository) FindAccount(
	ctx context.Context,
	nationalID string,
) (*PasswordResetAccount, error) {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("password reset repository is nil")
	}

	if nationalID == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("national id is empty")
	}

	account := &PasswordResetAccount{}

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		SELECT
			u.id,
			p.phone_number
		FROM users u
		INNER JOIN user_profiles up
			ON up.user_id = u.id
		LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
			SELECT phone_number
			FROM user_phones
			WHERE user_id = u.id
			  AND is_primary = TRUE
			ORDER BY created_at ASC
			LIMIT 1
		) p ON TRUE
		WHERE up.national_id = $1
		  AND u.status = 'active'
		`,
		nationalID,
	).Scan(
		&account.UserID,
		&account.PhoneNumber,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to find account for password reset: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if account.PhoneNumber == "" {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"primary phone number not found",
		)
	}

	return account, nil
}

func (r *PasswordResetRepository) CreateToken(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
) (
	string,
	time.Time,
	error,
) {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return "", time.Time{}, errors.New(
			"password reset repository is nil",
		)
	}

	if userID == "" {
		return "", time.Time{}, errors.New(
			"user id is empty",
		)
	}

	token, err := generatePasswordResetToken()

	if err != nil {
		return "", time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to generate reset token: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	tokenHash := hashPasswordResetToken(token)

	now := time.Now()
	expiresAt := now.Add(passwordResetTokenTTL)

	_, err = r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		INSERT INTO password_reset_tokens (
			user_id,
			token_hash,
			expires_at,
			created_at,
			updated_at
		)
		VALUES (
			$1,
			$2,
			$3,
			$4,
			$4
		)
		`,
		userID,
		tokenHash,
		expiresAt,
		now,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return "", time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to create password reset token: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return token, expiresAt, nil
}

func (r *PasswordResetRepository) FindValidToken(
	ctx context.Context,
	token string,
) (*PasswordResetToken, error) {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"password reset repository is nil",
		)
	}

	if token == "" {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"reset token is empty",
		)
	}

	tokenHash := hashPasswordResetToken(token)

	result := &PasswordResetToken{}

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		SELECT
			id,
			user_id,
			expires_at,
			used_at
		FROM password_reset_tokens
		WHERE token_hash = $1
		  AND used_at IS NULL
		`,
		tokenHash,
	).Scan(
		&result.ID,
		&result.UserID,
		&result.ExpiresAt,
		&result.UsedAt,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to find password reset token: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if !time.Now().Before(result.ExpiresAt) {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"password reset token expired",
		)
	}

	return result, nil
}

func (r *PasswordResetRepository) MarkUsed(
	ctx context.Context,
	tokenID string,
) error {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New(
			"password reset repository is nil",
		)
	}

	if tokenID == "" {
		return errors.New(
			"password reset token id is empty",
		)
	}

	now := time.Now()

	commandTag, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE password_reset_tokens
		SET
			used_at = $2,
			updated_at = $2
		WHERE id = $1
		  AND used_at IS NULL
		`,
		tokenID,
		now,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to mark password reset token used: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if commandTag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
		return errors.New(
			"password reset token not found or already used",
		)
	}

	return nil
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"account-backend/internal/verification"
	"golang.org/x/crypto/bcrypt"
)

var (
	ErrResetAccountNotFound = errors.New(
		"account not found",
	)

	ErrResetVerificationFailed = errors.New(
		"password reset verification failed",
	)

	ErrResetTokenInvalid = errors.New(
		"invalid password reset token",
	)

	ErrResetTokenExpired = errors.New(
		"password reset token expired",
	)

	ErrResetTokenUsed = errors.New(
		"password reset token already used",
	)

	ErrResetNewPasswordRequired = errors.New(
		"new password is required",
	)

	ErrResetNewPasswordTooShort = errors.New(
		"new password is too short",
	)
)

type PasswordResetService struct {
	repository      *PasswordResetRepository
	passwordRepo    *PasswordRepository
	sessionService  *SessionService
	trustedDevices  *TrustedDeviceRepository
	verification    *verification.Service
	defaultProvider string
}

func NewPasswordResetService(
	repository *PasswordResetRepository,
	passwordRepo *PasswordRepository,
	sessionService *SessionService,
	trustedDevices *TrustedDeviceRepository,
	verificationService *verification.Service,
	defaultProvider string,
) *PasswordResetService {
	return &PasswordResetService{
		repository:      repository,
		passwordRepo:    passwordRepo,
		sessionService:  sessionService,
		trustedDevices:  trustedDevices,
		verification:    verificationService,
		defaultProvider: defaultProvider,
	}
}

func (s *PasswordResetService) ForgotPassword(
	ctx context.Context,
	nationalID string,
	provider string,
	verificationCode string,
) (string, time.Time, error) {
	if s == nil ||
		s.repository == nil ||
		s.verification == nil {
		return "", time.Time{}, errors.New(
			"password reset service is nil",
		)
	}

	nationalID = strings.TrimSpace(nationalID)

	if nationalID == "" {
		return "", time.Time{}, ErrResetAccountNotFound
	}

	account, err := s.repository.FindAccount(
		ctx,
		nationalID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return "", time.Time{}, ErrResetAccountNotFound
	}

	if provider == "" {
		provider = s.defaultProvider
	}

	result, err := s.verification.ExecuteVerification(
		ctx,
		account.UserID,
		verification.VerificationTypeContact,
		provider,
		map[string]string{
			"contact_type":      "mobile",
			"contact_value":     account.PhoneNumber,
			"verification_code": verificationCode,
		},
	)

	if err != nil {
		return "", time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to execute password reset verification: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if result == nil ||
		result.Status != verification.VerificationStatusVerified {
		return "", time.Time{}, ErrResetVerificationFailed
	}

	return s.repository.CreateToken(
		ctx,
		account.UserID,
	)
}

func (s *PasswordResetService) ResetPassword(
	ctx context.Context,
	resetToken string,
	newPassword string,
) error {
	if s == nil ||
		s.repository == nil ||
		s.passwordRepo == nil ||
		s.sessionService == nil {
		return errors.New(
			"password reset service is nil",
		)
	}

	if resetToken == "" {
		return ErrResetTokenInvalid
	}

	if newPassword == "" {
		return ErrResetNewPasswordRequired
	}

	if len(newPassword) < minimumPasswordLength {
		return ErrResetNewPasswordTooShort
	}

	token, err := s.repository.FindValidToken(
		ctx,
		resetToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if strings.Contains(
			err.Error(),
			"expired",
		) {
			return ErrResetTokenExpired
		}

		return ErrResetTokenInvalid
	}

	newHash, err := bcrypt.GenerateFromPassword(
		[]byte(newPassword),
		bcrypt.DefaultCost,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to hash reset password: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if err := s.passwordRepo.UpdatePassword(
		ctx,
		token.UserID,
		string(newHash),
	); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if err := s.repository.MarkUsed(
		ctx,
		token.ID,
	); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if err := s.sessionService.LogoutAllDevices(
		ctx,
		token.UserID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to revoke sessions after password reset: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if s.trustedDevices != nil {
		if err := s.trustedDevices.RevokeAll(
			ctx,
			token.UserID,
		); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf(
				"failed to revoke trusted devices after password reset: %w",
				err,
			)
		}
	}

	return nil
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"golang.org/x/crypto/bcrypt"
)

var (
	ErrCurrentPasswordRequired = errors.New(
		"current password is required",
	)

	ErrNewPasswordRequired = errors.New(
		"new password is required",
	)

	ErrCurrentPasswordIncorrect = errors.New(
		"current password is incorrect",
	)

	ErrNewPasswordTooShort = errors.New(
		"new password is too short",
	)

	ErrNewPasswordSameAsCurrent = errors.New(
		"new password must be different from current password",
	)
)

const minimumPasswordLength = 8

type PasswordService struct {
	repository     *PasswordRepository
	sessionService *SessionService
}

func NewPasswordService(
	repository *PasswordRepository,
	sessionService *SessionService,
) *PasswordService {
	return &PasswordService{
		repository:     repository,
		sessionService: sessionService,
	}
}

func (s *PasswordService) ChangePassword(
	ctx context.Context,
	accessToken string,
	currentPassword string,
	newPassword string,
) error {
	if s == nil ||
		s.repository == nil ||
		s.sessionService == nil {
		return errors.New("password service is nil")
	}

	if currentPassword == "" {
		return ErrCurrentPasswordRequired
	}

	if newPassword == "" {
		return ErrNewPasswordRequired
	}

	if len(newPassword) < minimumPasswordLength {
		return ErrNewPasswordTooShort
	}

	if currentPassword == newPassword {
		return ErrNewPasswordSameAsCurrent
	}

	sessionResult, err := s.sessionService.ValidateAccessToken(
		ctx,
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to validate session: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if sessionResult == nil ||
		sessionResult.Session == nil ||
		!sessionResult.Valid {
		return ErrSessionNotFound
	}

	userID := sessionResult.Session.UserID

	currentHash, err := s.repository.GetPasswordHash(
		ctx,
		userID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if err := bcrypt.CompareHashAndPassword(
		[]byte(currentHash),
		[]byte(currentPassword),
	); err != nil {
		return ErrCurrentPasswordIncorrect
	}

	newHash, err := bcrypt.GenerateFromPassword(
		[]byte(newPassword),
		bcrypt.DefaultCost,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to hash new password: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if err := s.repository.UpdatePassword(
		ctx,
		userID,
		string(newHash),
	); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// Security rule:
	// Changing the password invalidates all existing sessions.
	if err := s.sessionService.LogoutAllDevices(
		ctx,
		userID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to revoke sessions after password change: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return nil
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
)

var ErrNationalIDAlreadyRegistered = errors.New(
	"national id already registered",
)

var ErrPhoneAlreadyRegistered = errors.New(
	"phone number already registered",
)

var ErrEmailAlreadyRegistered = errors.New(
	"email already registered",
)

type Repository struct {
	db *postgres.Client
}

func NewRepository(db *postgres.Client) *Repository {
	return &Repository{
		db: db,
	}
}

func (r *Repository) Register(
	ctx context.Context,
	request *RegistrationRequest,
	passwordHash string,
) (*RegistrationResponse, error) {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("auth repository is nil")
	}

	tx, err := r.db.Pool.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	defer tx.Rollback(ctx)

	var userID string
	var status string
	var createdAt time.Time

	userQuery := `
		INSERT INTO users (
			user_type
		)
		VALUES ($1)
		RETURNING
			id,
			status,
			created_at
	`

	err = tx.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		userQuery,
		request.UserType,
	).Scan(
		&userID,
		&status,
		&createdAt,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	profileQuery := `
		INSERT INTO user_profiles (
			user_id,
			first_name,
			last_name,
			national_id,
			birth_date,
			father_name,
			address,
			postal_code
		)
		VALUES (
			$1,
			$2,
			$3,
			$4,
			$5::date,
			$6,
			$7,
			$8
		)
	`

	_, err = tx.Exec(
		ctx,
		profileQuery,
		userID,
		request.FirstName,
		request.LastName,
		request.NationalID,
		request.BirthDate,
		request.FatherName,
		request.Address,
		request.PostalCode,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if strings.Contains(
			err.Error(),
			`user_profiles_national_id_key`,
		) {
			return nil, ErrNationalIDAlreadyRegistered
		}

		return nil, err
	}

	phoneQuery := `
		INSERT INTO user_phones (
			user_id,
			phone_number,
			is_primary,
			is_verified
		)
		VALUES (
			$1,
			$2,
			TRUE,
			FALSE
		)
	`

	_, err = tx.Exec(
		ctx,
		phoneQuery,
		userID,
		request.PhoneNumber,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if strings.Contains(
			err.Error(),
			`idx_user_phones_unique_number`,
		) {
			return nil, ErrPhoneAlreadyRegistered
		}

		return nil, err
	}

	if request.Email != "" {

		emailQuery := `
			INSERT INTO user_emails (
				user_id,
				email,
				is_primary,
				is_verified
			)
			VALUES (
				$1,
				$2,
				TRUE,
				FALSE
			)
		`

		_, err = tx.Exec(
			ctx,
			emailQuery,
			userID,
			request.Email,
		)

		if err != nil {
			if strings.Contains(
				err.Error(),
				`idx_user_emails_unique_email`,
			) {
				return nil, ErrEmailAlreadyRegistered
			}

			return nil, err
		}
	}

	credentialQuery := `
		INSERT INTO auth_credentials (
			user_id,
			password_hash
		)
		VALUES (
			$1,
			$2
		)
	`

	_, err = tx.Exec(
		ctx,
		credentialQuery,
		userID,
		passwordHash,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	if err := tx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return &RegistrationResponse{
		UserID:        userID,
		UserType:      request.UserType,
		Status:        status,
		PhoneVerified: false,
		CreatedAt:     createdAt,
	}, nil
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"regexp"
	"strings"

	"golang.org/x/crypto/bcrypt"
)

type Service struct {
	repository *Repository
}

func NewService(repository *Repository) *Service {
	return &Service{
		repository: repository,
	}
}

func (s *Service) Register(
	ctx context.Context,
	request *RegistrationRequest,
) (*RegistrationResponse, error) {

	if s == nil || s.repository == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("auth repository is nil")
	}

	if request == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("registration request is required")
	}

	request.UserType = strings.TrimSpace(request.UserType)
	request.NationalID = strings.TrimSpace(request.NationalID)
	request.FirstName = strings.TrimSpace(request.FirstName)
	request.LastName = strings.TrimSpace(request.LastName)
	request.FatherName = strings.TrimSpace(request.FatherName)
	request.BirthDate = strings.TrimSpace(request.BirthDate)
	request.PhoneNumber = strings.TrimSpace(request.PhoneNumber)
	request.Email = strings.TrimSpace(
		strings.ToLower(request.Email),
	)
	request.Address = strings.TrimSpace(request.Address)
	request.PostalCode = strings.TrimSpace(request.PostalCode)

	if request.UserType != "individual" {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"only individual registration is supported in auth v1",
		)
	}

	if !validNationalID(request.NationalID) {
		return nil, errors.New("invalid national id")
	}

	if request.FirstName == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("first name is required")
	}

	if request.LastName == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("last name is required")
	}

	if request.FatherName == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("father name is required")
	}

	if request.BirthDate == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("birth date is required")
	}

	if !validPhone(request.PhoneNumber) {
		return nil, errors.New("invalid phone number")
	}

	if request.Address == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("address is required")
	}

	if !validPostalCode(request.PostalCode) {
		return nil, errors.New("invalid postal code")
	}

	if len(request.Password) < 8 {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"password must be at least 8 characters",
		)
	}

	passwordHash, err := bcrypt.GenerateFromPassword(
		[]byte(request.Password),
		bcrypt.DefaultCost,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return s.repository.Register(
		ctx,
		request,
		string(passwordHash),
	)
}

func validNationalID(value string) bool {

	if !regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9]{10}$`).MatchString(value) {
		return false
	}

	allSame := true

	for i := 1; i < len(value); i++ {
		if value[i] != value[0] {
			allSame = false
			break
		}
	}

	if allSame {
		return false
	}

	sum := 0

	for i := 0; i < 9; i++ {
		sum += int(value[i]-'0') * (10 - i)
	}

	remainder := sum % 11
	check := int(value[9] - '0')

	if remainder < 2 {
		return check == remainder
	}

	return check == 11-remainder
}

func validPhone(value string) bool {
	return regexp.MustCompile(
		`^09[0-9]{9}$`,
	).MatchString(value)
}

func validPostalCode(value string) bool {
	return regexp.MustCompile(
		`^[0-9]{10}$`,
	).MatchString(value)
}

package auth

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

type SessionHandler struct {
	service *SessionService
}

func NewSessionHandler(
	service *SessionService,
) *SessionHandler {
	return &SessionHandler{
		service: service,
	}
}

type RefreshRequest struct {
	RefreshToken string `json:"refresh_token"`
}

type RefreshResponse struct {
	AccessToken  string `json:"access_token"`
	RefreshToken string `json:"refresh_token"`
	ExpiresIn    int64  `json:"expires_in"`
}

type SessionMessageResponse struct {
	Message string `json:"message"`
}

func (h *SessionHandler) Refresh(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)
		return
	}

	var request RefreshRequest

	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&request); err != nil {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusBadRequest,
			"invalid request body",
		)
		return
	}

	if request.RefreshToken == "" {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusBadRequest,
			"refresh_token is required",
		)
		return
	}

	accessToken,
		refreshToken,
		accessExpiresAt,
		err := h.service.Refresh(
		r.Context(),
		request.RefreshToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		switch {
		case errors.Is(err, ErrEmptyRefreshToken):
			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusBadRequest,
				"refresh token is required",
			)

		case errors.Is(err, ErrSessionNotFound),
			errors.Is(err, ErrSessionRevoked),
			errors.Is(err, ErrSessionExpired),
			errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidToken):
			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				"invalid refresh token",
			)

		default:
			log.Printf(
				"auth session refresh internal error: %v",
				err,
			)

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusInternalServerError,
				"internal server error",
			)
		}

		return
	}

	expiresIn := int64(
		time.Until(accessExpiresAt).Seconds(),
	)

	if expiresIn < 0 {
		expiresIn = 0
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		RefreshResponse{
			AccessToken:  accessToken,
			RefreshToken: refreshToken,
			ExpiresIn:    expiresIn,
		},
	)
}

func (h *SessionHandler) Logout(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)
		return
	}

	accessToken := extractBearerToken(
		r.Header.Get("Authorization"),
	)

	if accessToken == "" {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusUnauthorized,
			"access token is required",
		)
		return
	}

	err := h.service.Logout(
		r.Context(),
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		switch {
		case errors.Is(err, ErrEmptyAccessToken),
			errors.Is(err, ErrSessionNotFound),
			errors.Is(err, ErrSessionRevoked):
			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				"invalid access token",
			)

		default:
			log.Printf(
				"auth session logout internal error: %v",
				err,
			)

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusInternalServerError,
				"internal server error",
			)
		}

		return
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		SessionMessageResponse{
			Message: "logout successful",
		},
	)
}

func (h *SessionHandler) LogoutAllDevices(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)
		return
	}

	accessToken := extractBearerToken(
		r.Header.Get("Authorization"),
	)

	if accessToken == "" {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusUnauthorized,
			"access token is required",
		)
		return
	}

	result, err := h.service.ValidateAccessToken(
		r.Context(),
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		switch {
		case errors.Is(err, ErrSessionNotFound),
			errors.Is(err, ErrSessionRevoked),
			errors.Is(err, ErrSessionExpired),
			errors.Is(err, ErrEmptyAccessToken):
			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				"invalid access token",
			)

		default:
			log.Printf(
				"auth session validation internal error: %v",
				err,
			)

			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusInternalServerError,
				"internal server error",
			)
		}

		return
	}

	if result == nil ||
		result.Session == nil ||
		!result.Valid {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusUnauthorized,
			"invalid access token",
		)
		return
	}

	if err := h.service.LogoutAllDevices(
		r.Context(),
		result.Session.UserID,
	); err != nil {
		log.Printf(
			"auth logout all devices internal error: %v",
			err,
		)

		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusInternalServerError,
			"internal server error",
		)

		return
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		SessionMessageResponse{
			Message: "all devices logged out successfully",
		},
	)
}

func extractBearerToken(
	authorization string,
) string {
	authorization = strings.TrimSpace(authorization)

	if authorization == "" {
		return ""
	}

	const prefix = "Bearer "

	if !strings.HasPrefix(authorization, prefix) {
		return ""
	}

	return strings.TrimSpace(
		strings.TrimPrefix(authorization, prefix),
	)
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
)

const (
	accessTokenTTL  = 15 * time.Minute
	refreshTokenTTL = 30 * 24 * time.Hour
)

type SessionRepository struct {
	db *postgres.Client
}

type Session struct {
	ID               string
	UserID           string
	AccessTokenHash  string
	RefreshTokenHash string
	AccessExpiresAt  time.Time
	RefreshExpiresAt time.Time
	RevokedAt        *time.Time
	CreatedAt        time.Time
	UpdatedAt        time.Time
}

func NewSessionRepository(
	db *postgres.Client,
) *SessionRepository {
	return &SessionRepository{
		db: db,
	}
}

func generateSessionToken(size int) (string, error) {
	buffer := make([]byte, size)

	if _, err := rand.Read(buffer); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(buffer), nil
}

func hashSessionToken(token string) string {
	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(token))

	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(hash[:])
}

func (r *SessionRepository) Create(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
) (
	string,
	string,
	time.Time,
	error,
) {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return "", "", time.Time{}, errors.New(
			"session repository is nil",
		)
	}

	accessToken, err := generateSessionToken(32)
	if err != nil {
		return "", "", time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to generate access token: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	refreshToken, err := generateSessionToken(48)
	if err != nil {
		return "", "", time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to generate refresh token: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	accessHash := hashSessionToken(accessToken)
	refreshHash := hashSessionToken(refreshToken)

	now := time.Now()
	accessExpiresAt := now.Add(accessTokenTTL)
	refreshExpiresAt := now.Add(refreshTokenTTL)

	_, err = r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		INSERT INTO auth_sessions (
			user_id,
			access_token_hash,
			refresh_token_hash,
			access_expires_at,
			refresh_expires_at,
			created_at,
			updated_at
		)
		VALUES (
			$1,
			$2,
			$3,
			$4,
			$5,
			$6,
			$6
		)
		`,
		userID,
		accessHash,
		refreshHash,
		accessExpiresAt,
		refreshExpiresAt,
		now,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return "", "", time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to create auth session: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return accessToken,
		refreshToken,
		accessExpiresAt,
		nil
}

func (r *SessionRepository) FindByAccessToken(
	ctx context.Context,
	accessToken string,
) (*Session, error) {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"session repository is nil",
		)
	}

	if accessToken == "" {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"access token is empty",
		)
	}

	accessHash := hashSessionToken(accessToken)

	session := &Session{}

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		SELECT
			id,
			user_id,
			access_token_hash,
			refresh_token_hash,
			access_expires_at,
			refresh_expires_at,
			revoked_at,
			created_at,
			updated_at
		FROM auth_sessions
		WHERE access_token_hash = $1
		`,
		accessHash,
	).Scan(
		&session.ID,
		&session.UserID,
		&session.AccessTokenHash,
		&session.RefreshTokenHash,
		&session.AccessExpiresAt,
		&session.RefreshExpiresAt,
		&session.RevokedAt,
		&session.CreatedAt,
		&session.UpdatedAt,
	)

	if err != nil {

		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
			return nil, ErrSessionNotFound
		}

		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to find session by access token: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return session, nil
}
func (r *SessionRepository) FindByRefreshToken(
	ctx context.Context,
	refreshToken string,
) (*Session, error) {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"session repository is nil",
		)
	}

	if refreshToken == "" {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"refresh token is empty",
		)
	}

	refreshHash := hashSessionToken(refreshToken)

	session := &Session{}

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		SELECT
			id,
			user_id,
			access_token_hash,
			refresh_token_hash,
			access_expires_at,
			refresh_expires_at,
			revoked_at,
			created_at,
			updated_at
		FROM auth_sessions
		WHERE refresh_token_hash = $1
		`,
		refreshHash,
	).Scan(
		&session.ID,
		&session.UserID,
		&session.AccessTokenHash,
		&session.RefreshTokenHash,
		&session.AccessExpiresAt,
		&session.RefreshExpiresAt,
		&session.RevokedAt,
		&session.CreatedAt,
		&session.UpdatedAt,
	)

	if err != nil {

		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
			return nil, ErrSessionNotFound
		}

		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to find session by refresh token: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return session, nil
}
func (r *SessionRepository) Revoke(
	ctx context.Context,
	sessionID string,
) error {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New(
			"session repository is nil",
		)
	}

	if sessionID == "" {
		return errors.New(
			"session id is empty",
		)
	}

	now := time.Now()

	commandTag, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE auth_sessions
		SET
			revoked_at = COALESCE(revoked_at, $2),
			updated_at = $2
		WHERE id = $1
		`,
		sessionID,
		now,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to revoke auth session: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if commandTag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
		return errors.New(
			"auth session not found",
		)
	}

	return nil
}

func (r *SessionRepository) RevokeAll(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
) error {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New(
			"session repository is nil",
		)
	}

	if userID == "" {
		return errors.New(
			"user id is empty",
		)
	}

	now := time.Now()

	_, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE auth_sessions
		SET
			revoked_at = COALESCE(revoked_at, $2),
			updated_at = $2
		WHERE user_id = $1
		  AND revoked_at IS NULL
		`,
		userID,
		now,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to revoke all auth sessions: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return nil
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"
)

var (
	ErrSessionNotFound   = errors.New("session not found")
	ErrSessionRevoked    = errors.New("session revoked")
	ErrSessionExpired    = errors.New("session expired")
	ErrInvalidToken      = errors.New("invalid token")
	ErrEmptyAccessToken  = errors.New("access token is empty")
	ErrEmptyRefreshToken = errors.New("refresh token is empty")
)

type SessionService struct {
	repository *SessionRepository
}

func NewSessionService(
	repository *SessionRepository,
) *SessionService {
	return &SessionService{
		repository: repository,
	}
}

type SessionValidationResult struct {
	Session *Session
	Valid   bool
}

func (s *SessionService) ValidateAccessToken(
	ctx context.Context,
	accessToken string,
) (*SessionValidationResult, error) {
	if s == nil || s.repository == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("session service is nil")
	}

	if accessToken == "" {
		return nil, ErrEmptyAccessToken
	}

	session, err := s.repository.FindByAccessToken(
		ctx,
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to find session by access token: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if session == nil {
		return nil, ErrSessionNotFound
	}

	if session.RevokedAt != nil {
		return &SessionValidationResult{
			Session: session,
			Valid:   false,
		}, ErrSessionRevoked
	}

	if !time.Now().Before(session.AccessExpiresAt) {
		return &SessionValidationResult{
			Session: session,
			Valid:   false,
		}, ErrSessionExpired
	}

	return &SessionValidationResult{
		Session: session,
		Valid:   true,
	}, nil
}

func (s *SessionService) ValidateRefreshToken(
	ctx context.Context,
	refreshToken string,
) (*Session, error) {
	if s == nil || s.repository == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("session service is nil")
	}

	if refreshToken == "" {
		return nil, ErrEmptyRefreshToken
	}

	session, err := s.repository.FindByRefreshToken(
		ctx,
		refreshToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to find session by refresh token: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if session == nil {
		return nil, ErrSessionNotFound
	}

	if session.RevokedAt != nil {
		return nil, ErrSessionRevoked
	}

	if !time.Now().Before(session.RefreshExpiresAt) {
		return nil, ErrSessionExpired
	}

	return session, nil
}

func (s *SessionService) Refresh(
	ctx context.Context,
	refreshToken string,
) (
	string,
	string,
	time.Time,
	error,
) {
	if s == nil || s.repository == nil {
		return "", "", time.Time{}, errors.New(
			"session service is nil",
		)
	}

	session, err := s.ValidateRefreshToken(
		ctx,
		refreshToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return "", "", time.Time{}, err
	}

	if err := s.repository.Revoke(
		ctx,
		session.ID,
	); err != nil {
		return "", "", time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to revoke old session during refresh: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	accessToken, newRefreshToken, accessExpiresAt, err :=
		s.repository.Create(
			ctx,
			session.UserID,
		)

	if err != nil {
		return "", "", time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to create refreshed session: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return accessToken,
		newRefreshToken,
		accessExpiresAt,
		nil
}

func (s *SessionService) RevokeSession(
	ctx context.Context,
	sessionID string,
) error {
	if s == nil || s.repository == nil {
		return errors.New("session service is nil")
	}

	if sessionID == "" {
		return errors.New("session id is empty")
	}

	if err := s.repository.Revoke(
		ctx,
		sessionID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to revoke session: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return nil
}

func (s *SessionService) Logout(
	ctx context.Context,
	accessToken string,
) error {
	if s == nil || s.repository == nil {
		return errors.New("session service is nil")
	}

	if accessToken == "" {
		return ErrEmptyAccessToken
	}

	session, err := s.repository.FindByAccessToken(
		ctx,
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to find session during logout: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if session == nil {
		return ErrSessionNotFound
	}

	if session.RevokedAt != nil {
		return ErrSessionRevoked
	}

	if err := s.repository.Revoke(
		ctx,
		session.ID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to logout session: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return nil
}

func (s *SessionService) LogoutAllDevices(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
) error {
	if s == nil || s.repository == nil {
		return errors.New("session service is nil")
	}

	if userID == "" {
		return errors.New("user id is empty")
	}

	if err := s.repository.RevokeAll(
		ctx,
		userID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to logout all devices: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return nil
}

package auth

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"
)

type TrustedDeviceInfo struct {
	ID         string     `json:"id"`
	DeviceName string     `json:"device_name,omitempty"`
	ExpiresAt  time.Time  `json:"expires_at"`
	LastUsedAt *time.Time `json:"last_used_at,omitempty"`
	CreatedAt  time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
	RevokedAt  *time.Time `json:"revoked_at,omitempty"`
}

func (r *TrustedDeviceRepository) List(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
) ([]TrustedDeviceInfo, error) {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("trusted device repository is nil")
	}

	rows, err := r.db.Pool.Query(
		ctx,
		`
		SELECT
			id,
			device_name,
			expires_at,
			last_used_at,
			created_at,
			revoked_at
		FROM trusted_devices
		WHERE user_id = $1
		ORDER BY created_at DESC
		`,
		userID,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to list trusted devices: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	defer rows.Close()

	var devices []TrustedDeviceInfo

	for rows.Next() {

		var device TrustedDeviceInfo

		if err := rows.Scan(
			&device.ID,
			&device.DeviceName,
			&device.ExpiresAt,
			&device.LastUsedAt,
			&device.CreatedAt,
			&device.RevokedAt,
		); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf(
				"failed to scan trusted device: %w",
				err,
			)
		}

		devices = append(devices, device)
	}

	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to iterate trusted devices: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return devices, nil
}

func (r *TrustedDeviceRepository) GetByID(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
	deviceID string,
) (*TrustedDeviceInfo, error) {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("trusted device repository is nil")
	}

	if userID == "" || deviceID == "" {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"user id and device id are required",
		)
	}

	device := &TrustedDeviceInfo{}

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		SELECT
			id,
			device_name,
			expires_at,
			last_used_at,
			created_at,
			revoked_at
		FROM trusted_devices
		WHERE id = $1
		  AND user_id = $2
		`,
		deviceID,
		userID,
	).Scan(
		&device.ID,
		&device.DeviceName,
		&device.ExpiresAt,
		&device.LastUsedAt,
		&device.CreatedAt,
		&device.RevokedAt,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to get trusted device: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return device, nil
}

	userID string,

) error {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {

		SET
			revoked_at = COALESCE(revoked_at, $3),

		`,
		deviceID,
		userID,
		now,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to revoke trusted device: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if commandTag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
		return errors.New(
			"trusted device not found",
		)
	}

	return nil
}

func (r *TrustedDeviceRepository) RevokeAll(

	if err != nil {

	}

}

func (r *TrustedDeviceRepository) RevokeAllExcept(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
	deviceID string,
) error {
	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return errors.New("trusted device repository is nil")
	}

	now := time.Now()

	_, err := r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE trusted_devices
		SET
			revoked_at = COALESCE(revoked_at, $3),
			updated_at = $3
		WHERE user_id = $1
		  AND id <> $2
		  AND revoked_at IS NULL
		`,
		userID,
		deviceID,
		now,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to revoke other trusted devices: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return nil
}

type TrustedDeviceManagementService struct {
	repository     *TrustedDeviceRepository
	sessionService *SessionService
}

func NewTrustedDeviceManagementService(
	repository *TrustedDeviceRepository,
	sessionService *SessionService,
) *TrustedDeviceManagementService {
	return &TrustedDeviceManagementService{
		repository:     repository,
		sessionService: sessionService,
	}
}

func (s *TrustedDeviceManagementService) userIDFromAccessToken(
	ctx context.Context,
	accessToken string,
) (string, error) {
	result, err := s.sessionService.ValidateAccessToken(
		ctx,
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	if result == nil ||
		result.Session == nil ||
		!result.Valid {
		return "", ErrSessionNotFound
	}

	return result.Session.UserID, nil
}

func (s *TrustedDeviceManagementService) List(
	ctx context.Context,
	accessToken string,
) ([]TrustedDeviceInfo, error) {
	if s == nil ||
		s.repository == nil ||
		s.sessionService == nil {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"trusted device management service is nil",
		)
	}

	userID, err := s.userIDFromAccessToken(
		ctx,
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return s.repository.List(ctx, userID)
}

func (s *TrustedDeviceManagementService) Get(
	ctx context.Context,
	accessToken string,
	deviceID string,
) (*TrustedDeviceInfo, error) {
	if s == nil ||
		s.repository == nil ||
		s.sessionService == nil {
		return nil, errors.New(
			"trusted device management service is nil",
		)
	}

	userID, err := s.userIDFromAccessToken(
		ctx,
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return s.repository.GetByID(
		ctx,
		userID,
		deviceID,
	)
}

func (s *TrustedDeviceManagementService) Revoke(
	ctx context.Context,
	accessToken string,
	deviceID string,
) error {
	if s == nil ||
		s.repository == nil ||
		s.sessionService == nil {
		return errors.New(
			"trusted device management service is nil",
		)
	}

	userID, err := s.userIDFromAccessToken(
		ctx,
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	return s.repository.RevokeDevice(
		ctx,
		userID,
		deviceID,
	)
}

func (s *TrustedDeviceManagementService) RevokeAll(
	ctx context.Context,
	accessToken string,
) error {
	if s == nil ||
		s.repository == nil ||
		s.sessionService == nil {
		return errors.New(
			"trusted device management service is nil",
		)
	}

	userID, err := s.userIDFromAccessToken(
		ctx,
		accessToken,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	return s.repository.RevokeAll(
		ctx,
		userID,
	)
}

package auth

import (
	"errors"
	"log"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
)

type TrustedDeviceManagementHandler struct {
	service *TrustedDeviceManagementService
}

func NewTrustedDeviceManagementHandler(
	service *TrustedDeviceManagementService,
) *TrustedDeviceManagementHandler {
	return &TrustedDeviceManagementHandler{
		service: service,
	}
}

func (h *TrustedDeviceManagementHandler) List(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodGet {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)
		return
	}

	token := extractBearerToken(
		r.Header.Get("Authorization"),
	)

	if token == "" {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusUnauthorized,
			"access token is required",
		)
		return
	}

	devices, err := h.service.List(
		r.Context(),
		token,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if isSessionAuthError(err) {
			writeJSONError(
				w,
				http.StatusUnauthorized,
				"invalid access token",
			)
			return
		}

		log.Printf(
			"trusted device list internal error: %v",
			err,
		)

		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusInternalServerError,
			"internal server error",
		)

		return
	}

	if devices == nil {
		devices = []TrustedDeviceInfo{}
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		map[string]interface{}{
			"devices": devices,
		},
	)
}

func (h *TrustedDeviceManagementHandler) HandleDevice(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	token := extractBearerToken(
		r.Header.Get("Authorization"),
	)

	if token == "" {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusUnauthorized,
			"access token is required",
		)
		return
	}

	path := strings.TrimPrefix(
		r.URL.Path,
		"/api/v1/auth/devices/",
	)

	path = strings.Trim(path, "/")

	if path == "" {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusBadRequest,
			"device id is required",
		)
		return
	}

	parts := strings.Split(
		path,
		"/",
	)

	deviceID := parts[0]

	if len(parts) == 1 &&
		r.Method == http.MethodGet {

		device, err := h.service.Get(
			r.Context(),
			token,
			deviceID,
		)

		if err != nil {
			h.writeDeviceError(
				w,
				err,
			)
			return
		}

		writeJSON(
			w,
			http.StatusOK,
			device,
		)

		return
	}

	if len(parts) == 2 &&
		parts[1] == "revoke" &&
		r.Method == http.MethodPost {

		err := h.service.Revoke(
			r.Context(),
			token,
			deviceID,
		)

		if err != nil {
			h.writeDeviceError(
				w,
				err,
			)
			return
		}

		writeJSON(
			w,
			http.StatusOK,
			map[string]string{
				"message": "trusted device revoked successfully",
			},
		)

		return
	}

	writeJSONError(
		w,
		http.StatusNotFound,
		"device endpoint not found",
	)
}

func (h *TrustedDeviceManagementHandler) RevokeAll(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	r *http.Request,
) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusMethodNotAllowed,
			"method not allowed",
		)
		return
	}

	token := extractBearerToken(
		r.Header.Get("Authorization"),
	)

	if token == "" {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusUnauthorized,
			"access token is required",
		)
		return
	}

	err := h.service.RevokeAll(
		r.Context(),
		token,
	)

	if err != nil {
		h.writeDeviceError(
			w,
			err,
		)
		return
	}

	writeJSON(
		w,
		http.StatusOK,
		map[string]string{
			"message": "all trusted devices revoked successfully",
		},
	)
}

func (h *TrustedDeviceManagementHandler) writeDeviceError(
	w http.ResponseWriter,
	err error,
) {
	if isSessionAuthError(err) {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusUnauthorized,
			"invalid access token",
		)
		return
	}

	if errors.Is(err, ErrSessionNotFound) {
		writeJSONError(
			w,
			http.StatusNotFound,
			"trusted device not found",
		)
		return
	}

	log.Printf(
		"trusted device management internal error: %v",
		err,
	)

	writeJSONError(
		w,
		http.StatusInternalServerError,
		"internal server error",
	)
}

func isSessionAuthError(err error) bool {
	return errors.Is(err, ErrEmptyAccessToken) ||
		errors.Is(err, ErrSessionNotFound) ||
		errors.Is(err, ErrSessionRevoked) ||
		errors.Is(err, ErrSessionExpired)
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: account-backend\internal\auth\trusted_device_repository.go
SIZE: 2816 bytes
====================================================================================================
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/base64"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"

	"account-backend/infrastructure/postgres"
)

const trustedDeviceTTLSeconds = 90 * 24 * 60 * 60

type TrustedDeviceRepository struct {
	db *postgres.Client
}

func NewTrustedDeviceRepository(
	db *postgres.Client,
) *TrustedDeviceRepository {
	return &TrustedDeviceRepository{
		db: db,
	}
}

func generateTrustedDeviceToken() (string, error) {

	buffer := make([]byte, 32)

	if _, err := rand.Read(buffer); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(buffer), nil
}

func hashTrustedDeviceToken(token string) string {

	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(token))

	return base64.RawURLEncoding.EncodeToString(hash[:])
}

func (r *TrustedDeviceRepository) FindValid(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
	token string,
) (bool, error) {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return false, errors.New(
			"trusted device repository is nil",
		)
	}

	token = strings.TrimSpace(token)

	if token == "" {
		return false, nil
	}

	tokenHash := hashTrustedDeviceToken(token)

	var exists bool

	err := r.db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		SELECT EXISTS (
			SELECT 1
			FROM trusted_devices
			WHERE user_id = $1
			  AND token_hash = $2
			  AND revoked_at IS NULL
			  AND expires_at > NOW()
		)
		`,
		userID,
		tokenHash,
	).Scan(&exists)

	if err != nil {
		return false, fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to check trusted device: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	if exists {
		_, err = r.db.Pool.Exec(
			ctx,
			`
			UPDATE trusted_devices
			SET
				last_used_at = NOW(),
				updated_at = NOW()
			WHERE user_id = $1
			  AND token_hash = $2
			  AND revoked_at IS NULL
			`,
			userID,
			tokenHash,
		)

		if err != nil {
			return false, fmt.Errorf(
				"failed to update trusted device usage: %w",
				err,
			)
		}
	}

	return exists, nil
}

func (r *TrustedDeviceRepository) Create(
	ctx context.Context,
	userID string,
	deviceName string,
) (string, error) {

	if r == nil || r.db == nil || r.db.Pool == nil {
		return "", errors.New(
			"trusted device repository is nil",
		)
	}

	token, err := generateTrustedDeviceToken()

	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	tokenHash := hashTrustedDeviceToken(token)

	_, err = r.db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		INSERT INTO trusted_devices (
			user_id,
			device_name,
			token_hash,
			expires_at,
			last_used_at
		)
		VALUES (
			$1,
			$2,
			$3,
			NOW() + INTERVAL '90 days',
			NOW()
		)
		`,
		userID,
		deviceName,
		tokenHash,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf(
			"failed to create trusted device: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	return token, nil
}

	if err := expectHTTPStatus(
		client,
		server.URL+"/test?scope_id="+assetB1.ID,
		accessToken,
		http.StatusForbidden,
		"Project A ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ Asset B1",
	); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Switch role assignment to Asset A1
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	if err := updateAssignmentScope(
		ctx,
		db,
		roleAssignmentID,
		assetA1.ID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"switch assignment to Asset A1: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// 8-9 Asset A1 scope
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	if err := expectHTTPStatus(
		client,
		server.URL+"/test?scope_id="+assetA1.ID,
		accessToken,
		http.StatusOK,
		"Asset A1 ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ Asset A1",
	); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if err := expectHTTPStatus(
		client,
		server.URL+"/test?scope_id="+projectA.ID,
		accessToken,
		http.StatusForbidden,
		"Asset A1 ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ Project A",
	); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Switch role assignment to Global
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	if err := updateAssignmentScope(
		ctx,
		db,
		roleAssignmentID,
		global.ID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"switch assignment to Global: %w",
			err,
		)
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// 10 Global scope
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	if err := expectHTTPStatus(
		client,
		server.URL+"/test?scope_id="+assetB1.ID,
		accessToken,
		http.StatusOK,
		"Global ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ Asset B1",
	); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// --------------------------------------------------------
	// Cleanup
	// --------------------------------------------------------

	cleanupAuthorizationData(
		ctx,
		db,
		roleAssignmentID,
		assetB1.ID,
		assetA1.ID,
		projectB.ID,
		projectA.ID,
		organization.ID,
		global.ID,
	)

	cleanupDone = true

	fmt.Println()
	fmt.Println("[PASS] Authorization scope hierarchy verified end-to-end.")
	return nil
}

func loadAccessToken() (string, error) {
	token := strings.TrimSpace(
		os.Getenv("AUTHORIZATION_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN"),
	)

	if token != "" {
		return token, nil
	}

	path := filepath.Join(
		".tmp",
		"authorization-access-token.txt",
	)

	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return "",
			fmt.Errorf(
				"AUTHORIZATION_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN is not set and %s could not be read: %w",
				path,
				err,
			)
	}

	token = strings.TrimSpace(string(data))

	if token == "" {
		return "",
			errors.New("authorization access token is empty")
	}

	return token, nil
}

func createScope(
	ctx context.Context,
	db *postgres.Client,
	scopeType authorization.ScopeType,
	resourceID string,
	name string,
	description string,
	parentScopeID *string,
) (testScope, error) {

	var result testScope

	err := db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		`
		INSERT INTO authorization_scopes (
			scope_type,
			resource_type,
			resource_id,
			parent_scope_id,
			name,
			description,
			is_active
		)
		VALUES (
			$1,
			$2,
			$3,
			$4,
			$5,
			$6,
			TRUE
		)
		RETURNING
			id::text,
			scope_type
		`,
		scopeType,
		scopeResourceType(scopeType),
		nullableResourceID(resourceID),
		parentScopeID,
		name,
		description,
	).Scan(
		&result.ID,
		&result.Type,
	)

	if err != nil {
		return result,
			fmt.Errorf(
				"create scope %s: %w",
				name,
				err,
			)
	}

	result.Resource = resourceID
	result.Name = name

	return result, nil
}

func scopeResourceType(scopeType authorization.ScopeType) any {
	switch scopeType {
	case authorization.ScopeTypeOrganization:
		return "organization"

	case authorization.ScopeTypeProject:
		return "project"

	case authorization.ScopeTypeAsset:
		return "asset"

	default:
		return nil
	}
}

func nullableResourceID(value string) any {
	if value == "" {
		return nil
	}

	return value
}

func updateAssignmentScope(
	ctx context.Context,
	db *postgres.Client,
	assignmentID string,
	scopeID string,
) error {

	_, err := db.Pool.Exec(
		ctx,
		`
		UPDATE authorization_user_roles
		SET
			scope_id = $1,
			updated_at = NOW()
		WHERE id = $2
		`,
		scopeID,
		assignmentID,
	)

	return err
}

func queryString(
	ctx context.Context,
	db *postgres.Client,
	query string,
	args ...any,
) (string, error) {

	var value string

	err := db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		query,
		args...,
	).Scan(&value)

	return value, err
}

func queryInt(
	ctx context.Context,
	db *postgres.Client,
	query string,
	args ...any,
) (int, error) {

	var value int

	err := db.Pool.QueryRow(
		ctx,
		query,
		args...,
	).Scan(&value)

	return value, err
}

func expectHTTPStatus(
	client *http.Client,
	url string,
	accessToken string,
	expected int,
	label string,
) error {

	request, err := http.NewRequest(
		http.MethodGet,
		url,
		nil,
	)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"%s: create request: %w",
			label,
			err,
		)
	}

	request.Header.Set(
		"Authorization",
		"Bearer "+accessToken,
	)

	response, err := client.Do(request)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"%s: HTTP request failed: %w",
			label,
			err,
		)
	}

	defer response.Body.Close()

	_, _ = io.Copy(io.Discard, response.Body)

	if response.StatusCode != expected {
		return fmt.Errorf(
			"[FAIL] %s -> expected HTTP %d, got HTTP %d",
			label,
			expected,
			response.StatusCode,
		)
	}

	fmt.Printf(
		"[PASS] %-32s -> HTTP %d\n",
		label,
		response.StatusCode,
	)

	return nil
}

func cleanupAuthorizationData(
	ctx context.Context,
	db *postgres.Client,
	roleAssignmentID string,
	assetB1ID string,
	assetA1ID string,
	projectBID string,
	projectAID string,
	organizationID string,
	globalID string,
) {

	if roleAssignmentID != "" {
		_, _ = db.Pool.Exec(
			ctx,
			`
			DELETE FROM authorization_user_roles
			WHERE id = $1
			`,
			roleAssignmentID,
		)
	}

	scopeIDs := []string{
		assetB1ID,
		assetA1ID,
		projectBID,
		projectAID,
		organizationID,
		globalID,
	}

	for _, scopeID := range scopeIDs {
		if scopeID == "" {
			continue
		}

		_, _ = db.Pool.Exec(
			ctx,
			`
			DELETE FROM authorization_scopes
			WHERE id = $1
			`,
			scopeID,
		)
	}
}

func newUUIDLike() string {
	data := make([]byte, 16)

	if _, err := rand.Read(data); err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}

	data[6] = (data[6] & 0x0f) | 0x40
	data[8] = (data[8] & 0x3f) | 0x80

	hexValue := hex.EncodeToString(data)

	return fmt.Sprintf(
		"%s-%s-%s-%s-%s",
		hexValue[0:8],
		hexValue[8:12],
		hexValue[12:16],
		hexValue[16:20],
		hexValue[20:32],
	)
}
